Conclusion: 该协议给出了量子麦克斯韦妖的物理透明实现，指针充当量子测量辅件，其位置与电池能量相关联；方案适用于囚禁离子系统，有助于理解量子相干性与测量在热力学中的作用。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for extracting work from a coherent quantum battery state by exploiting measurement-assisted feedback mediated by a continuous-variable pointer. The scheme relies on the unitary operator $U = \exp(-i k t \, \hat{H} \otimes \hat{P}/\hbar)$, which generates entanglement between the battery's energy eigenstates and the position of an auxiliary pointer. A subsequent projective measurement of the pointer's position conditionally prepares the battery in a pure state from which work can be extracted via a feedback unitary. We analyze the protocol for a two-level quantum battery and a Gaussian pointer, computing the conditional states and the corresponding daemonic ergotropy. For the pure initial state considered, we find that the daemonic ergotropy equals the standard ergotropy for all interaction strengths, demonstrating that the measurement-assisted feedback recovers the full extractable work that would otherwise become inaccessible due to entanglement with the pointer when its degrees of freedom are traced out. The protocol thus provides a physically transparent realization of a quantum Maxwell demon, where the pointer acts as a quantum measurement ancilla whose position becomes correlated with the battery's energy. The scheme is amenable to experimental implementation in trapped-ion systems, and it contributes to the ongoing efforts to understand the role of quantum coherence and measurement in thermodynamics.

</details>


### [96] [Subspace Controllability in Variational Quantum Circuits: Maximising Expressivity and Increasing Search Efficiency with Dynamical Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24475)
*Andrew Rowan Barlow,Hans-Martin Rieser,Markus Lange*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究子空间可控性是否对经典任务有效，通过在MNIST-1D多分类任务中使用可控线路，发现大多数达到最低训练与验证损失的量子模型均为子空间可控，且优于超参数搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分混合量子模型在量子硬件上实现机器学习存在训练困难，根源在于变分量子电路形成的损失景观问题。已有研究将子空间可控性概念扩展以提升量子任务中寻找全局最小值的成功率，但其是否适用于经典任务、以及相比超参数搜索是否具有优势尚不明确。

Method: 在MNIST-1D多分类任务中采用可控电路进行实验，并将结果与超参数搜索进行对比。

Result: 在超参数搜索下，多数取得最低训练损失和验证损失的量子模型都是子空间可控的。

Conclusion: 超参数搜索可被限制在这类子空间可控模型上，从而降低计算成本和时间。

Abstract: Variational hybrid quantum models are a common paradigm for realising machine learning on quantum hardware. Nonetheless, they are challenging to train due to several problems intrinsic to the loss landscapes formed by variational quantum circuits. To mitigate these issues, researchers have extended the idea of subspace controllability leading to an increase in the success rate of variational models in finding the global minimum for quantum-based tasks. However, it remains unknown whether these results extend to classical-based tasks, and if any advantages are realised over a hyperparameter search. We begin to address this question by employing controllable circuits in a multi- classification task using MNIST-1D. Our results show that over a hyperparameter search, the majority of quantum models that achieve the lowest training and validation losses are subspace controllable. These results indicate that the hyperparameter search can be restricted to such models, in turn reducing computational cost and time.

</details>


### [97] [Statistical mechanics of multipartite entanglement in hypergraph states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24508)
*Paolo Scarafile,Giorgia Trotta,Paolo Facchi,Giuseppe Magnifico,Giorgio Parisi,Saverio Pascazio,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过统计力学框架，将超图态的二分纯度映射为经典二进制自旋哈密顿量，解析或数值地统计超图态集合中的最大多体纠缠态数量及其纠缠分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究特定纯n量子比特超图态家族中的多体纠缠，借助统计力学方法刻画纠缠态在受限量子态集合中的统计分布规律。

Method: 将平均二分纯度映射为2^n个经典二进制自旋的有效哈密顿量：每个超图态对应一个经典自旋构型，温度作为控制参量，高温对应随机超图态的均匀系综，零温对应最大多体纠缠态（MMES），零温熵的指数即给出MMES的数目；小系统（n≤5）精确枚举刻画能量图景与热力学量，大系统（n=6,7）采用模拟退火与并行回火算法采样指数大状态空间。

Result: 精确枚举完整刻画了小系统的能量图景与热力学可观测量，验证了已知的MMES计数；对大系统定量预测了MMES的数量，并揭示纠缠在超图态集合中的统计分布方式。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了超图态作为利用热力学方法研究多体纠缠的理想平台，在受限量子态家族的纠缠结构方面兼具计算进展与物理洞见。

Abstract: We investigate multipartite entanglement in a particular family of pure $n$-qubit hypergraph states through a statistical-mechanics framework, where the average bipartite purity maps onto an effective Hamiltonian of $2^n$ classical binary spins. In this correspondence, each hypergraph state uniquely corresponds to a classical spin configuration, while temperature serves as a control parameter that continuously interpolates between a uniform ensemble of random hypergraph states at high temperature and maximally multipartite entangled states (MMES) at zero temperature. Remarkably, the exponential of the zero-temperature entropy directly gives the number of MMES within the set of hypergraph states. For small system sizes ($n \leq 5$), we perform an exact enumeration, fully characterizing the energy landscape and associated thermodynamic observables, and validating known MMES counts. For larger systems ($n = 6$ and $7$), where exact methods become computationally infeasible, we employ simulated annealing and parallel tempering algorithms to efficiently sample the exponentially large state space. Our analysis yields quantitative predictions of the number of MMES and reveals how entanglement is statistically distributed across the sets of hypergraph states. These results establish hypergraph states as an ideal platform for investigating multipartite entanglement through thermodynamic methods, offering both computational advances and physical insights into the structure of quantum entanglement in restricted families of quantum states.

</details>


### [98] [A Reconfigurable Multilayer Quantum Key Distribution Network over Existing Metropolitan Fibre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24561)
*Mariella Minder,Andreas Siakolas,Elizabeth Pasatembou,Stylianos Mavrikos,Stephanos Yerolatsitis,Konstantinos Katzis,Kyriacos Kalli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个部署于现网城域光纤上的七节点多层QKD网络，通过光谱管理、可重构光交换与网状密钥管理层实现资源复用，在73天内生成119.4 Gbit密钥材料并达到99.1%链路可用性，揭示了跨层耦合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络的规模化受限于光纤与设备资源，且在"棕地"部署场景下必须围绕既有经典电信基础设施和QKD技术能力进行工程设计，因此需要一套能够在继承约束下扩展端到端服务可达性的架构。

Method: 在现有城域光纤上部署七节点多层QKD网络：采用光谱管理技术应对各层继承约束；将可信节点环与一个三节点子网通过可重构光交换互连，在不增加发射机的前提下扩展物理连接与服务可达性；并构建网状密钥管理层，将端点请求映射到可信中继路径，为L1、L3及一次性密码本应用提供密钥。

Result: 在73天运行期内，八种链路配置共生成119.4 Gbit密钥材料，链路可用率达99.1%；随机化端到端请求暴露出密钥消耗、存储密钥状态与量子层重配置之间的间接跨层耦合。

Conclusion: 通过可重构性、光谱复用与逻辑密钥管理抽象，可实现量子层资源的共享，为在既有电信基础设施上扩展量子网络服务可达性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling quantum networks requires architectures extending end-to-end service reachability despite constrained fibre and equipment. Critically, in brownfield deployments, the quantum network must be engineered around classical telecommunications infrastructure and the operating capabilities of quantum key distribution (QKD) technology. To address this, we demonstrate a seven-node, multilayer QKD network deployed over existing metropolitan fibre. The implementation compares spectral management techniques to accommodate inherited constraints across its layers. It combines a trusted-node ring with a three-node subnetwork interconnected through reconfigurable optical switching, extending physical connectivity and service reachability without an additional transmitter. A meshed key management layer maps endpoint requests onto trusted-relay paths and supplies Layer 1, Layer 3, and one-time-pad applications. Over 73 days, eight link configurations generated 119.4 Gbit of secret key material with 99.1% link availability. Randomised end-to-end requests revealed indirect cross-layer coupling between key consumption, stored-key state and quantum-layer reconfiguration, demonstrating resource sharing through reconfigurability, spectral multiplexing and logical key-management abstraction.

</details>


### [99] [An Exact Operator Formulation of Statistical Quasiparticle Theory for Nonlinear System-Bath Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24603)
*Yu Su,Yao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出开放量子系统的统计准粒子理论算符表述，将耗散子（dissipaton）显式构造为热场浴空间中的集体算符，突破标准高斯影响泛函的线性耦合限制，并借助递归广义Wick定理处理非线性耦合，直接由Liouville方程生成层级运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯影响泛函与耗散子代数主要局限于系统-浴线性耦合情形，难以统一处理非线性耦合下的非微扰、非马尔可夫动力学，因此需要建立更一般的算符化统计准粒子理论。

Method: 针对高斯浴，在热场浴空间中显式构造耗散子集体算符，为耗散子代数提供微观算符基；通过递归广义Wick定理纳入非线性相互作用，使多项式耦合对应的层级方程可自动生成，并直接从Liouville方程导出层级运动方程。

Result: 建立了显式浴算符表示与非微扰、非马尔可夫动力学之间的联系，实现了线性与非线性系统-浴相互作用的统一处理，并能自动生成多项式耦合的层级方程。

Conclusion: 该框架扩展了统计准粒子理论至非线性系统-浴耦合，为耗散子代数提供了微观算符基础，并统一了线性与非线性开放量子系统动力学的处理方式。

Abstract: We develop an operator formulation of statistical quasiparticle theory for open quantum systems with nonlinear system-bath coupling, extending beyond the linear coupling setting of the standard Gaussian influence functional formulation. For Gaussian baths, the statistical quasiparticles, termed dissipatons, are explicitly constructed as collective operators in the thermofield bath space. Our construction provides a microscopic operator basis for the dissipaton algebra and generates hierarchical equations of motion directly from the Liouville equation. Nonlinear interactions are incorporated through a recursive generalized Wick's theorem, enabling automatic generation of hierarchical equations for polynomial couplings. The resulting framework connects explicit bath operator representations to nonperturbative, non-Markovian dynamics and unifies the treatment of linear and nonlinear system-bath interactions.

</details>


### [100] [Explicit closed-form propagators for time-dependent gain-loss master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24617)
*Léonce Dupays*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对含时速率主方程中的时间排序难题，在特定对易子代数封闭条件下给出了时序与反时序传播子的显式闭式因子分解，并应用于增益-损耗Lindblad方程，得到任意含时增益/损耗速率下的精确动力学映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时速率主方程中的时间排序是驱动开放系统动力学的核心障碍，传统Wei-Norman分解需数值求解耦合非线性微分方程组，缺少直接的闭式因子分解方法。

Method: 设生成元 L(t)=α(t)A+β(t)B，且要求 [A,B] 同时是 A、B 的伴随作用的特征算符，即 [A,[A,B]]=λ_A[A,B] 与 [B,[A,B]]=λ_B[A,B]。在此代数封闭条件下，直接确定因子分解系数，从而显式构造时序与反时序传播子，无需解非线性微分方程组。随后将结果应用于玻色增益-损耗Lindblad方程。

Result: 获得任意含时增益与损耗速率下的精确动力学映射；通过推导初始Fock态Wigner函数的时间演化并与独立的相空间结果对比完成基准验证；进一步计算了驱动增益-损耗动力学下二项式玻色码的码空间生存概率。

Conclusion: 所提构造绕开了Wei-Norman分解的非线性方程求解，在不依赖数值积分微分方程组的条件下给出含时开放系统传播子的闭式因子分解，为增益-损耗主导的玻色量子系统与玻色码性能分析提供了精确解析工具。

Abstract: Time ordering is a central obstacle in driven open-system dynamics governed by master equations with time-dependent rates. We derive explicit closed-form factorizations of the chronological and antichronological propagators for a time-dependent generator of the form $\mathcal{L}(t)=α(t)A+β(t)B$, when the commutator $[A,B]$ is a simultaneous eigenoperator of the adjoint actions of $A$ and $B$, namely $[A,[A,B]]=λ_{A}[A,B]$ and $[B,[A,B]]=λ_{B}[A,B]$. In contrast to the disentangling procedure known as the Wei-Norman decomposition, our construction determines the factorization coefficients directly, without solving a coupled system of nonlinear differential equations. Applying this result to the bosonic gain-loss Lindblad equation, we obtain the exact dynamical map for arbitrary time-dependent gain and loss rates. The gain-loss master equation provides a canonical description of a driven harmonic oscillator coupled to a thermal bath. We benchmark the construction by deriving the time evolution of the Wigner function of an initial Fock state and comparing it with independent phase-space results. We further apply the resulting dynamical map to evaluate the code-space survival probability of a binomial bosonic code under driven gain-loss dynamics.

</details>


### [101] [Phase space anatomy of dynamical quantum phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24636)
*Zakaria Mzaouali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示离散相空间中动力学量子相变（DQPT）的两种定义——序参量转变与回归率非解析性——在所需信息尺度上的本质分离：前者由局域约化态决定，无需准概率负性；后者可包含所有真约化态都缺失的全局信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中DQPT通常有两种判据：晚期序参量行为，或量子态回归率的非解析性。作者质疑二者是否等价，并希望借助离散相空间厘清二者究竟依赖何种尺度的信息。

Method: 以离散相空间（准概率表示）为框架，从信息尺度上区分两类转变；对stabilizer回归，推导回归率的精确分解，将其拆分为支撑损失代价与破坏性量子干涉代价两部分；并以qutrit Potts链的动力学作为具体模型进行验证。

Result: 序参量转变仅由局域约化态决定，不依赖准概率负性；而一般全局回归可携带任何真约化态都不含的信息。qutrit Potts链显示选择性相消能逆转竞争性分块回归的排序并延迟其交换；精确控制还给出在交叉点处负性为零的热力学回归尖点。

Conclusion: 回归奇异性的存在性与干涉在分支选择中的作用是两个不同的物理问题。序参量转变与回归率奇异不应被混为一谈，离散相空间从信息尺度上为二者划出清晰边界。

Abstract: Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPT) are commonly defined either by the behavior of a late-time order parameter or by nonanalyticities in a quantum state's return rate. We show that discrete phase space fundamentally separates these two notions by the scale of information they require. An order parameter transition is determined by a local reduced state, requiring no quasiprobability negativity. In contrast, general global returns can contain information absent from every proper reduced state. For stabilizer returns, we derive an exact decomposition of the rate into distinct costs from loss of support and destructive quantum interference. Dynamics of a qutrit Potts chain show that selective cancellation can reverse the ranking of competing block returns and delay their exchange. An exact control also exhibits a thermodynamic return cusp with zero negativity at the crossing. The existence of a return singularity and the role of interference in selecting its branches are therefore distinct physical questions.

</details>


### [102] [Circuit Hypernetworks for Quantum-Augmented Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24657)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Mengyang Xiong,Jun Dai,Bang Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: HyperQ在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型上加入token条件的量子残差分支，仅训练新增的轻量级量子线路超网络，通过具有线性评估代价的精确经典期望值表达训练16至64量子比特线路，在更少微调数据下显著提升下游基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子线路引入大语言模型是一种有吸引力的适配思路，但在大型模型内部评估较宽的量子线路计算代价高昂，难以训练。论文旨在探索一种可扩展、计算可承受的量子增强语言建模架构方案。

Method: 提出HyperQ，在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型主干上为每个transformer块添加token条件的量子残差分支。每个分支读取token隐藏状态，由轻量级线路超网络输出该token特有的旋转角、耦合强度与测量轴，在共享稀疏线路结构中执行并测量，将测量值经残差连接加回。主干保持冻结，仅训练新增分支。所需期望值具有精确的经典表达式，其评估代价随量子比特数线性增长，从而支持在1.1B参数量主干内训练16至64量子比特的线路。

Result: 随线路宽度增加，各下游基准的平均得分由47.65提升至54.30；在64量子比特时，HyperQ分别超过冻结主干及其低秩适配（LoRA）对应版本4.71和3.67分。HyperQ仅用2万条提示-响应对微调，而经典基线使用了20万条。

Conclusion: 结果表明，token条件的量子线路发射是一种可行且计算可承受的量子增强语言建模架构途径，可在冻结主干上以极少训练数据获得优于经典适配方法的性能。

Abstract: Language models can be adapted by changing the computations applied to individual tokens. Quantum circuits offer one such approach, but evaluating wider circuits inside a large model can be computationally demanding. Here we introduce HyperQ, which adds token-conditioned quantum residual branches to a frozen masked-diffusion language model. A quantum residual branch is a module in each transformer block that reads a token's hidden state, emits the coordinates of that token's circuit, executes it, and adds the measured values back through a residual connection. The backbone remains frozen, and only the added branches are trained. Within each branch, a lightweight circuit hypernetwork emits token-specific rotation angles, coupling strengths, and measurement axes in a shared sparse circuit structure. The required expectation values have an exact classical expression whose evaluation cost grows linearly with the qubit count, enabling circuits from 16 to 64 qubits to be trained within a 1.1-billion-parameter backbone. Across downstream benchmarks, increasing circuit width raises the average score from 47.65 to 54.30. At 64 qubits, HyperQ exceeds the backbone and its low-rank-adapted counterpart by 4.71 and 3.67 points, respectively. HyperQ is fine-tuned on 20,000 prompt-response pairs, compared with 200,000 for the classical baselines. These findings support token-conditioned circuit emission as a tractable architectural approach to quantum-augmented language modelling.

</details>


### [103] [Experimental evidence of generalization in quantum machine learning in small-data regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24666)
*Leena Anthony,Artemiy Burov,Nicolas Piro,Matteo Dal Peraro,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了硬件兼容的量子卷积神经网络（QCNN，含中电路测量与前馈），在极少样本的手写数字二分类任务中验证其可学性，并系统评估从2×2到512×512分辨率的编码扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像、临床试验与罕见病等场景普遍面临数据稀缺问题。QCNN因层级结构与强归纳偏置、参数量随系统规模仅对数增长而受到关注；Caro等（2022）的泛化界表明泛化误差随可训练参数量而非希尔伯特空间维度增长，暗示QCNN可能处于样本高效区间，但其实际少样本学习能力与可扩展性尚待实证检验。

Method: 开发支持中电路测量与经典前馈的硬件兼容QCNN；在手写数字二分类任务上与参数量匹配（45参数）的经典卷积网络及约25,000参数的无约束经典基线对比；将振幅编码与角度编码电路在2×2至512×512像素分辨率下进行转译，考察其深度与量子比特开销；并在医学动机的BreastMNIST基准上验证。

Result: 仅需10个训练样本即可获得较强测试性能，且泛化误差随训练集增大而下降；在45参数预算下QCNN可学习，而同等规模的经典网络停留在随机水平，但数据充足时约25,000参数的无约束经典基线仍最强。编码扩展实验显示：振幅编码量子比特高效但电路深度急剧增长，角度编码深度浅但量子比特需求变为不可承受。BreastMNIST上QCNN未超越无约束经典网络，但以少数个数量级的参数持续优于随机水平。

Conclusion: 对QCNN而言，少样本学习在实践中可以实现；但扩展到真实图像数据的主要制约不在优化层面，而在于数据编码方式与硬件执行能力。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning is a promising paradigm for learning from limited data, a central bottleneck in domains such as medical imaging, clinical trials, and rare diseases. Quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) are particularly attractive in this setting, combining a hierarchical architecture with strong inductive bias and a parameter count that grows only logarithmically with system size. Their appeal rests on the generalization bounds of Caro et al. (2022), which show that the generalization error of a quantum model scales with the number of trainable parameters rather than with the Hilbert-space dimension, placing QCNNs in a potentially sample-efficient regime. We develop a hardware-compatible QCNN with mid-circuit measurement and classical feed-forward, and show on a binary handwritten-digit task that strong test performance is achievable from as few as 10 training samples, with the generalization error decreasing as the training set grows. At a matched 45-parameter budget the QCNN learns where an equally small classical convolutional network stays at chance, although an unconstrained classical baseline with roughly 25,000 parameters remains strongest when data are plentiful. Transpiling amplitude and angle encoded circuits across image resolutions from 2x2 to 512x512 pixels then exposes the dominant scaling bottleneck: amplitude encoding stays qubit-efficient but grows extremely deep, whereas angle encoding stays shallow but becomes qubit-prohibitive. On the medically motivated BreastMNIST benchmark the QCNN does not surpass the unconstrained classical network, yet it learns consistently above chance using orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Our results indicate that for QCNNs, learning from few samples is attainable in practice, whereas scaling to realistic image data is constrained less by optimization than by data encoding and hardware execution.

</details>


### [104] [Wigner-positive quantum states can have lower entropy than the vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24670)
*Zacharie Van Herstraeten,Nicolas J. Cerf,Ulysse Chabaud*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了简单解析反例，推翻了Wigner熵猜想及其更强的Wigner主序化猜想，并揭示了不确定性与非高斯性之间的微妙相互作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner熵猜想断言纯高斯态最小化非负Wigner函数的Shannon熵。验证或证伪该猜想（及其更强的Wigner主序化猜想）对量子信息与相空间量子力学基础具有根本意义。

Method: 将一个特定的纯Wigner负态与另一恰当选取的量子态混合，使Wigner函数恢复非负性，同时保持Wigner熵严格略低于真空熵；据此推导出简单解析反例。

Result: 成功证伪Wigner熵猜想及更强的Wigner主序化猜想，熵界违反量不超过10^{-3}；此类亚真空Wigner熵态可任意接近真空，并可在0<α<2区间表现出亚真空Wigner-Rényi α-熵。物理机制源于不确定性原理与非高斯性的微妙相互作用。

Conclusion: Wigner熵猜想不成立，纯高斯态并非非负Wigner函数Shannon熵的最小化者；亚真空Wigner熵态确实存在，其根源是量子不确定性与非高斯性的协同效应。论文核心思想借助AI工具、基于极端非负Wigner函数的刻画发展而来。

Abstract: The Wigner entropy conjecture posits that pure Gaussian states minimize the Shannon entropy of non-negative Wigner functions, known as the Wigner entropy. We show that mixing a specific pure Wigner-negative state with another suitably chosen quantum state can restore Wigner positivity while retaining a Wigner entropy that is slightly - but strictly - below the vacuum entropy. As a consequence, we disprove the Wigner entropy conjecture, as well as the stronger Wigner majorization conjecture, by deriving simple analytical counterexamples with violations of the entropy bound no larger than $10^{-3}$. Such states with sub-vacuum Wigner entropy can be found arbitrarily close to the vacuum and can also exhibit sub-vacuum Wigner-Rényi $α$-entropy for $0<α<2$. We identify the physical mechanism behind the existence of such states, which arises from a subtle interplay between the uncertainty principle and non-Gaussianity. The key idea of this paper was developed with the help of AI tools, building on the characterization of extreme non-negative Wigner functions.

</details>


### [105] [Unified spatiotemporal quantum states and spatiotemporal entanglement from Kirkwood-Dirac phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24680)
*Zhian Jia,Mei-Hui Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间，给出时空量子态的统一刻画，建立其与OTOC、KMS条件及时空纠缠的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子态概念局限于空间域，而量子事件在时空中的关联需要时空量子态的框架；现有时空量子态表述多样且分散，缺乏统一刻画，且其与非经典性、时间性及纠缠的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 以时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间为核心工具，分析由Kirkwood-Dirac分布得到的非厄米、非正规时空态与由Margenau-Hill分布得到的厄米时空态；通过(准)概率混合实现统一表述；推导时空态的递归表达式；将构造推广到多重关联函数并联系OTOC；进一步建立基于s参数化时空态(准)概率混合的通用框架，研究Petz时间反演及两时刻KMS条件；最后用互补熵度量刻画时空纠缠。

Result: Kirkwood-Dirac时空态一般为非厄米、非正规，而Margenau-Hill时空态为厄米；(准)概率混合可涵盖几乎现有全部时空量子态表述；获得时空态递归表达式，阐明Kirkwood-Dirac非经典性与时间性的关系；推广至多重关联函数并关联OTOC；建立s参数化统一框架并给出Petz时间反演与两时刻KMS条件的应用；实现对时空纠缠的熵度量刻画。

Conclusion: 时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间为时空量子态提供了统一且具推广性的刻画框架，连通了非经典性、时间性、OTOC、KMS条件与时空纠缠等多个概念，为时空量子信息研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The notion of a spatiotemporal quantum state extends the conventional concept of a spatial quantum state to the spatiotemporal domain. Such states are represented by unit-trace operators that encode correlations among quantum events distributed across space and time. In this work, we use the spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac phase space to provide a unified characterization of spatiotemporal quantum states. Spatiotemporal states obtained from Kirkwood-Dirac distributions are generally non-Hermitian and nonnormal; whereas those constructed from Margenau-Hill distributions are Hermitian. We provide a unification via (quasi)probabilistic mixture of Kirkwwod-Dirac spatiotemporal states which encompasses almost all existing formulations of spatiotemporal quantum states. We derive recursive expressions for spatiotemporal states and elucidate the relation between Kirkwood-Dirac nonclassicality and the temporality of spatiotemporal states. We further extend the construction to many-fold correlation functions and establish its connection with out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs). We also develop a more general unifying framework based on (quasi)probabilistic mixture of $s$-parametrized spatiotemporal states and establish their Petz time reversal and application in studying Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) condition in two-time setting. Finally, we apply this framework to characterize quantum entanglement in spacetime and analyze spatiotemporal entanglement using several complementary entropy measures.

</details>


### [106] [Superconducting qubit based on altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24759)
*Xue-Feng Pan,Xin-Lei Hei,Franco Nori,Peng-Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究超导体-交错磁体-超导体（S-A-S）约瑟夫森结，通过器件设计实现可编程电流-相位关系，并提出一类基于交错磁性的类transmon超导量子比特，兼具大非谐性与抗退相干能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体具有零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂，有望用于下一代约瑟夫森器件。作者希望利用交错磁性实现约瑟夫森势的可编程调控，并据此设计高性能超导量子比特。

Method: 在S-A-S结中利用交错磁性调控约瑟夫森效应，工程设计所需的电流-相位关系；基于所得到的可编程约瑟夫森势，构建通过相干双库珀对隧穿工作的类transmon量子比特；在2φ结区间利用宇称保护机制，并通过磁通进行调控与偏置优化。

Result: 该类量子比特可同时实现大非谐性；在2φ结区间具备对电荷噪声的内在保护（宇称保护）；在合适偏置下可进一步抑制电荷噪声与磁通噪声；磁通可精确调控量子比特。

Conclusion: 交错磁体可作为约瑟夫森势工程的多功能平台，为兼具高相干性、大非谐性与宽可调性的高性能超导量子比特开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Altermagnets, characterized by vanishing net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provide a promising platform for next-generation Josephson devices. Here, we exploit the Josephson effect in superconductor-altermagnet-superconductor junctions and show how to engineer prescribed current-phase relations by device design. Based on these programmable Josephson potentials utilizing altermagnetism, we propose a new class of transmon-like superconducting qubits that combine large anharmonicity with enhanced robustness against decoherence via coherent two-Cooper-pair tunneling. We show that in the $2φ$-junction regime, this kind of qubit provides intrinsic protection against charge noise due to parity protection. Magnetic flux can be used to precisely control the qubit and, under appropriate bias, this architecture further suppresses charge and flux noise. Our results establish altermagnets as a versatile platform for Josephson-potential engineering and open a new route toward high-performance superconducting qubits combining high coherence, large anharmonicity, and broad tunability.

</details>


### [107] [Observable-targeted variational quantum simulation of Hamiltonian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24794)
*Leonardo Zambrano,Luciano Pereira,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出以目标可观测量为导向的变分量子模拟方法，仅更新参数以再现选定期望值，通过误差恒等式引导可观测量构造，在同等测量预算下延长目标误差容限内的模拟时间最高达4.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准变分量子模拟旨在复现全量子态的演化，但许多应用只需少数可观测量的期望值，全态复现造成不必要的测量与计算开销。

Method: 针对纯态哈密顿量动力学，设计更新电路参数以复现选定期望值演化的变分方法；利用精确误差恒等式指导可观测量选择，并基于目标与哈密顿量的重复对易子进行构造。对于泡利可观测量与泡利旋转电路，更新估计无需辅助量子比特或受控操作来估计重叠。

Result: 在六比特自旋、费米子与分子基准上，相比标准变分量子模拟，在相同测量次数预算下，目标误差容限内的中位模拟时间最高延长4.2倍。

Conclusion: 将变分更新导向目标可观测量可在不增加每时间步测量成本的前提下延长精确模拟时间，为面向应用的量子动力学模拟提供高效途径。

Abstract: Standard variational quantum simulation seeks to reproduce the evolution of the full quantum state, although many applications require only the expectation values of a few observables. We study a variational method for pure-state Hamiltonian dynamics that updates circuit parameters to reproduce the evolution of selected expectation values. An exact error identity guides the choice of observables, motivating a construction based on repeated commutators of the target with the Hamiltonian. For Pauli observables and Pauli-rotation circuits, the update can be estimated without ancillary qubits or controlled operations for overlap estimation. Across six-qubit spin, fermionic, and molecular benchmarks, the targeted update extends the median time within the target-error tolerance by up to a factor of $4.2$ relative to standard variational quantum simulation at equal shot budgets. These results show that directing the variational update toward the target observable can extend accurate simulation without increasing the measurement cost per time step.

</details>


### [108] [Stabilizing temporal quantum-enhanced sensitivity via sub-optimal measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24805)
*Wangsheng Zheng,Yaoling Yang,Abolfazl Bayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了多参数非平衡量子传感的普适时间标度律，揭示次优测量下精度呈受有界振荡调制的二次标度，并提出分组分时测量协议以消除振荡、稳定二次标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强灵敏度通常以精度随时间的超线性标度为核心资源，但这依赖复杂且含时依赖的最优测量，实际中难以实现；次优测量往往无法保持鲁棒的超线性标度并出现振荡，因此亟需在次优测量条件下仍能稳定获得超线性精度提升的方案。

Method: 首先在一般多参数非平衡量子传感框架下建立普适的时间标度律；进而刻画次优测量下传感精度的普适行为，即由额外有界振荡函数调制的二次标度；随后提出将测量划分为不同分组、各组在不同时刻执行的协议，以消除振荡项，从而在多参数情形下用次优测量获得鲁棒二次标度；并通过三个不同实例以及贝叶斯估计进行验证。

Result: 确立了普适时间标度律；发现次优测量下的精度表现为受有界振荡调制的二次标度；所提出的分组分时测量协议可获得稳定的鲁棒二次标度，且在多参数情形下对次优测量有效；该结论经三个不同实例与贝叶斯估计验证，并适用于任意有信息量的测量。

Conclusion: 该协议适用于每一种有信息量的测量，能在次优测量与多参数情形下稳健地实现二次标度精度提升，是迈向量子增强灵敏度实际应用的关键一步。

Abstract: In non-equilibrium probes, quantum-enhanced sensitivity is quantified through super-linear scaling of precision with respect to time, as a central metrology resource. However, this requires optimal measurements, which are often complex and time-dependent, making it challenging in practice. Sub-optimal measurements typically fail to retain robust super-linear scaling and show oscillations. Here, we begin by establishing a universal temporal scaling law in a general multi-parameter non-equilibrium quantum sensing framework. Within this setting, we identify the universal behavior of the sensing precision with sub-optimal measurements which shows quadratic scaling modulated by an additional bounded oscillatory function. To remove the oscillatory part, we propose a protocol in which measurements are partitioned into different groups, each performed at different times. The collective precision obtained from this protocol stabilizes a robust quadratic scaling even for sub-optimal measurements in the multi-parameter regime. We validate our protocol through three distinct examples as well as Bayesian estimation. Our protocol is applicable to every informative measurement and takes a key step towards practical realization of quantum-enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [109] [Trade-offs and experimental feasibility of nonlocal polygamy with two-outcome Bell inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24835)
*Josep Batle,Tomasz Rybotycki,Tomasz Białecki,Piotr Gawron,Adam Bednorz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多体量子系统中纠缠与贝尔非定域性在子系统间的不同共享约束，考察了双方各自具有二结果可观测量、每方2或3个测量设置下的贝尔不等式同时违反，识别出可在IBM Quantum硬件上演示同时违反的构型，并推导了若干多夫配置的界与权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与贝尔非定域性在多体系统子系统间具有不同的共享约束（如单配性与多夫性），理解这些约束的数学极限并寻找可在实际量子硬件上验证的实例具有理论与实验双重意义。

Method: 以二结果可观测量为对象，考虑每方2个或3个测量设置的贝尔不等式，构造并识别多方构型，分析其同时违反的可能性，并结合量子计算机实验能力推导多夫配置的界与权衡关系。

Result: 识别出若干可在IBM Quantum硬件上实现的同时违反贝尔不等式的构型，并给出若干多夫构型的界限与权衡关系。

Conclusion: 研究将量子计算机的实验能力与量子关联极限的数学方法相连接，为在多体系统中检验纠缠与非定域性的共享结构提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Entanglement and Bell nonlocality have different sharing constraints across subsystems of a multipartite quantum system. We examine simultaneous violations of Bell inequalities with two-outcome observables and two or three measurement settings per party. We identify configurations whose simultaneous violations can be demonstrated on IBM Quantum hardware, and derive bounds and trade-off relations for several polygamous configurations. The results connect experimental capabilities of quantum computers with mathematical methods for studying the limits of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [110] [Explanation of the Observed Energy Exchange through the vacuum in Optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24900)
*Vincenzo Macrì,Franco Nori,Alessandro Ferreri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出标准大失谐近似忽略的高阶光力相互作用可自然产生声子-声子耦合，从而微观解释真空介导的热传递实验，并据此提出可提取净功的循环协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fong等（Nature 576, 243 (2019)）实验中观察到机械薄膜间的真空介导热传递，但标准大失谐近似仅保留辐射压力相互作用、忽略高阶镜-场相互作用，无法给出微观解释，其物理起源一直存在争议。

Method: 采用全量子模型，将此前被忽略的高阶光力相互作用纳入标准光力框架进行解析处理。

Result: 被忽略的高阶光力相互作用自然生成声子-声子耦合，并定量重现实验观测到的能量交换；进一步提出由声子-声子相互作用驱动的循环过程，可实现净功提取。

Conclusion: 研究确立了高阶光力相互作用的基础性作用，为超越线性近似、描述下一代光力实验提供了微观理论框架。

Abstract: In cavity optomechanics, the standard large-detuning regime is commonly described by retaining only the radiation-pressure interaction, while higher-order mirror--field interactions are assumed to be negligible. This approximation, however, fails to provide a microscopic explanation for the vacuum-mediated heat transfer observed between the mechanical membranes in the experiment of Fong, et.al.,[Nature \textbf{576}, 243 (2019)], whose physical origin has remained under active debate. Here, using a fully quantum model, we show that the neglected higher-order optomechanical interactions naturally generate phonon-phonon coupling and quantitatively account for the observed energy exchange within the standard optomechanical framework. Building on this microscopic description, we further propose a protocol in which phonon-phonon interaction drives a cyclic process enabling net work extraction. Our results establish the fundamental role of higher-order optomechanical interactions and provide a microscopic framework for describing next-generation optomechanical experiments beyond the linear approximation.

</details>


### [111] [A thermal microwave bus for neutral atom quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24933)
*Matthew J. H. Kendall,Christopher J. Watson,Michael Ben Shem,Jonathan D. Breeze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种四能级架构，利用微波腔耦合里德堡态实现长程腔介导的两比特门，并评估其在热噪声与衰减下的保真度及其对量子纠错码的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列中的高保真两比特门依赖里德堡阻塞，其本质上是短程的，需借助原子穿梭才能实现长程连通，这带来额外开销。因此需要一种能直接提供长程连通性的门机制。

Method: 提出四能级架构：基态比特提供长寿命，里德堡态与微波腔耦合以实现长程腔介导门。先研究两种既有协议（Tavis-Cummings 色散 iSWAP 与驱动腔产生的受控相位门）的热依赖性，在里德堡衰减、热光子、有限腔线宽下仿真并给出闭式保真度界。随后引入双色拉曼门，仅虚拟布居里德堡态并抵消色散位移，以抑制原子与腔衰减。最后考虑微波腔内的光镊阵列。

Result: 双色拉曼门实现保真度 F = 0.997。在光镊阵列中，用腔介导门闭合 toric code 的周期边界，可将纠错轮次缩短 2.3 倍（实际阵列规模）；推广至 Bivariate bicycle 码，可将 gross code 的一轮缩短 4.8 倍。

Conclusion: 微波腔介导的虚拟里德堡门为中性原子阵列提供长程连通，兼具高保真度，并能显著加速量子纠错循环，适用于多种量子纠错码。

Abstract: High-fidelity two-qubit gates in neutral-atom arrays rely on the Rydberg blockade, which is intrinsically short ranged and requires long range connectivity to be achieved through atom shuttling. We propose a four level architecture, where the typical ground state qubit can be leveraged for its long lifetime and the Rydberg states couple to a microwave cavity, allowing for long range cavity mediated gates. We first find the thermal dependence of two established protocols, the dispersive iSWAP native to the Tavis-Cummings model and the Controlled phase gate generated from driving the cavity. We simulate them under the presence of Rydberg decay, thermal photons, finite cavity linewidth and find fidelities which accompany closed form bounds. We then introduce the bichromatic Raman gate, which only virtually populates the Rydberg states and cancels dispersive shifts to mitigate both atomic and cavity decay, achieving a fidelity of $F = 0.997$. Finally, we consider a full optical tweezer array in a microwave cavity, and show that using a cavity mediated gate to close the periodic boundaries of the toric code shortens an error correction round by a factor of 2.3 for realistic array sizes. This was then applied to the wider family of Bivariate bicycle codes, and we find that it shortens a round of the gross code by a factor of 4.8.

</details>


### [112] [Efficient Heralding of Loss-Tolerant Photonic GHZ States for Device-Independent Conference Key Agreement over Long Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24941)
*Yazeed K. Alwehaibi,Makoto Ishihara,Shakib Daryanoosh,Ewan Mer,Shang Yu,Wojciech Roga,Ian A. Walmsley,Masahiro Takeoka,Raj B. Patel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究损耗量子网络中设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA）的预示多体纠缠分发，发现计算基GHZ态（真空与n光子叠加）比固定光子数GHZ态具有更低临界探测效率，并提出异质源星型网络协议直接预示真空-n-光子GHZ态，实现O(η_c^{n/2})的长距离标度，可在现有探测器效率下完成DI-CKA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预示多体纠缠分发是实现损耗量子网络上设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA）的关键前提。尽管在无损耗时局域等价，不同单轨光子数编码的GHZ态在损耗容忍度上可能存在显著差异，因此需要寻找更抗损耗的编码方案与源架构。

Method: 论文比较计算基GHZ态（真空与n光子相干叠加）与固定光子数GHZ态，推导两类态临界探测效率的精确解析条件；提出使用异质源的星型网络协议直接预示真空-n-光子GHZ态；对四用户情形刻画光子损耗下的预示态，分析理想Pauli测量与基于位移的可及测量，并讨论SPDC实现中源致不对称对奇偶-CHSH检验中测量角色分配的影响。

Result: 计算基GHZ态可在显著更低探测效率下实现无探测漏洞的奇偶-CHSH违背；协议具有O(η_c^{n/2})的长距离标度（η_c为信道透射率）；四用户情形下可调源参数使真实多体纠缠在任意有限信道距离保持；在现有光电探测器可达效率下可实现DI-CKA，且密钥率与通信距离与既有预示方案相当。

Conclusion: 光子数编码、源架构与测量角色分配是抗损耗多体量子网络及提升DI-CKA性能的关键设计参数；计算基编码结合异质源星型网络为实现探测漏洞自由、探测效率可行的DI-CKA提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Heralded multipartite entanglement distribution is a key requirement for device-independent conference key agreement (DI-CKA) over lossy quantum networks. Although locally equivalent in the absence of loss, different single-rail photon-number encodings of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states can exhibit substantially different loss tolerance. We show that computational-basis GHZ states, comprising a coherent superposition of vacuum and an $n$-photon component, enable detection-loophole-free parity-CHSH violations at markedly lower detection efficiencies than previously considered fixed-photon-number GHZ states, and derive exact analytical conditions for the critical detection efficiencies of both state classes. Motivated by this advantage, we introduce a star-network protocol using heterogeneous sources to directly herald vacuum-$n$-photon GHZ states with long-distance scaling $O(η_{\mathrm{c}}^{n/2})$, where $η_{\mathrm{c}}$ is the channel transmittance. For four users, we characterize the heralded state under photon loss and show that tunable source parameters preserve genuine multipartite entanglement at any finite channel distance. For both ideal Pauli measurements and experimentally accessible displacement-based measurements, our protocol enables DI-CKA at detection efficiencies achievable with current photodetectors, while retaining key rates and communication distances comparable to those of previous heralded schemes. We discuss physical implementations and analyze an SPDC-based realization, showing that source-induced asymmetry can make measurement-role assignment in the parity-CHSH test crucial. These results identify photon-number encoding, source architecture, and measurement-role assignment as design parameters for loss-tolerant multipartite quantum networks and enhanced DI-CKA performance.

</details>


### [113] [Tight entropy contraction beyond detailed balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24953)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为超越细致平衡的有限维量子信道建立了定量熵收缩界：对与忠实条件期望相容的信道，GNS范数收缩可导出相对熵收缩（维度常数仅对数损失）；对具忠实渐近条件期望的量子马尔可夫半群，给出完全修正对数Sobolev常数相对GNS谱隙的下界，且允许初始环境纠缠、与外部系统规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典/量子熵收缩理论多依赖细致平衡假设，限制了对非平衡信道与开放系统（含初始纠缠、环境耦合）的分析；需要在不假设细致平衡的条件下建立可量化的熵收缩与谱隙关系，并保证界对环境规模不敏感。

Method: 以GNS（Gelfand–Naimark–Segal）构造与忠实条件期望为工具，将GNS范数收缩与相对熵收缩相联系（维度常数仅对数损失）；对具忠实渐近条件期望的量子马尔可夫半群，用GNS谱隙控制完全修正对数Sobolev常数。

Result: 证明了GNS范数收缩蕴含相对熵收缩，维度代价仅对数级；得到完全修正对数Sobolev常数下界由GNS谱隙给出；界允许初始与环境纠缠且不依赖环境系统尺寸；对交替储层脉冲与连续耦合能量阶梯给出尺寸依赖最优的界。

Conclusion: 在超越细致平衡的框架下建立了GNS谱隙与熵收缩/对数Sobolev常数的定量联系，为开放量子系统提供与环境规模无关的收敛界，并在具体模型中获得最优尺寸依赖性。

Abstract: We establish quantitative entropy contraction bounds for finite-dimensional quantum channels beyond detailed balance. For channels compatible with a faithful conditional expectation, we show that GNS (Gelfand--Naimark--Segal) norm contraction yields relative entropy contraction with only a logarithmic loss in the dimensional constant. For quantum Markov semigroups with a faithful asymptotic conditional expectation, this gives a lower bound on the complete modified logarithmic Sobolev constant in terms of the GNS spectral gap. The bounds allow initial entanglement with an external environment and are independent of its system size. Applications to alternating reservoir pulses and continuously coupled energy ladders give bounds with optimal size dependence.

</details>

### [114] [Vanadium doping induced valley asymmetries in WS$_2$ monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22580)
*Frederico B. Sousa,Boyang Zheng,Elizabeth Grace Houser,Paulo E. Faria Junior,Zhuohang Yu,Alessandra Ames,Gabriel A. D. Souza,Mingzu Liu,Gilmar Eugenio Marques,Leandro M. Malard,Mauricio Terrones,Vincent H. Crespi,Marcio D. Teodoro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文报道了钒掺杂WS₂单层中的谷不对称性：在给定磁极化下，一个谷具有更大的塞曼斜率与圆偏振度，且掺杂样品的整体圆偏振度约为纯净WS₂的两倍。DFT计算表明，自旋相关杂化导致的导带边能量位移差异造成两谷激子能量不同，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD单层可利用谷自由度编码与操控信息，但实际应用仍需对谷极化效率和谷塞曼效应实现更高级别的调控。近期以自旋极化金属原子作为替位缺陷打破时间反演对称性、诱导室温铁磁序并增强谷依赖光学响应，为谷调控提供了新途径。

Method: 对钒掺杂的WS₂单层进行谷相关光学测量，提取两谷的塞曼斜率与圆偏振度；结合密度泛函理论计算掺杂结构中的能带与激子能量，分析自旋相关杂化对导带边能量位移的影响。

Result: 在给定磁极化下，钒掺杂WS₂单层的两个谷表现出不对称性：一个谷的塞曼斜率更大、圆偏振度更高；掺杂样品的整体圆偏振度约为纯净WS₂单层的两倍。DFT计算显示，自旋相关杂化引起导带边不同的能量位移，进而导致两谷间激子能量不同，与实验观测相符。

Conclusion: 钒掺杂可在WS₂单层中引入谷不对称性并显著增强谷相关光学响应，其机制源于自旋相关杂化导致的谷间激子能量差异。该结果为基于缺陷工程二维材料的谷电子学技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers offer an innovative platform for encoding and manipulating information through the valley degree of freedom. While unique valley-related physical phenomena have been reported so far, practical applications still require advanced control over the valley polarization efficiency and the valley Zeeman effect. Recently, the introduction of spin-polarized metal atoms as substitutional defects was reported to break the time-reversal symmetry in TMD monolayers, consequently inducing a room-temperature ferromagnetic ordering and enhancing the valley-dependent optical responses. Here, we report valley asymmetries for vanadium-doped WS$_2$ monolayers. With a given magnetic polarization, one valley exhibits larger Zeeman slope and degree of circular polarization than the other valley. Additionally, the overall degree of circular polarization in the doped samples is approximately twice that of the pristine WS$_2$ monolayer. Density functional theory calculations in the doped structure show that different energy shifts in conduction band edges due to spin-dependent hybridization lead to different exciton energies between valleys, which is consistent with the experimental observations. Our results pave the way for valleytronic technologies based on defect-engineered two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [115] [Electrical Probing of Dark Excitons through Microwave Permittivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22710)
*Alex Stram,Zhida Liu,Ziheng Zhang,Yangchen He,Xuejian Ma,Kyoung Pyo Lee,Lisa Frammolino,Fuxiang Chen,Robert J. Boyd,Sirapas Tangton,Anand Swain,Kan Yao,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yuebing Zheng,Chih-Kang Shih,Daniel Rhodes,Li Yang,Xiaoqin Li,Keji Lai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文利用激光照明的微波阻抗显微镜,发现单层二硫化钨中的光激发激子可在千兆赫兹频段产生大且光学可调的介电响应,从而以无接触电学方式在纳米尺度成像暗激子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄半导体中的激子通常只能通过光学信号探测,因为其寿命短,一般认为无法进行电学检测。作者希望突破这一限制,找到一种无需接触的电学探测途径,特别是针对难以光学观测的暗激子。

Method: 采用激光照明的微波阻抗显微镜,对单层二硫化钨进行测量;对比高纯度封装样品与缺陷丰富样品的响应差异;通过扩散的空间成像和亚线性功率依赖关系分析主导贡献者;并用第一性原理计算激子极化率以验证测量到的磁化率。

Result: 高纯度封装样品表现出与激子谱共振的纯介电响应,而缺陷丰富样品则由传统光电导主导;空间扩散成像与亚线性功率依赖表明长寿命暗激子是主要贡献者;第一性原理计算的激子极化率与实测磁化率吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作确立激子可作为光学可调的介电元件,并将微波显微镜引入为直接电学探测暗激子输运的手段,空间分辨率优于100纳米。

Abstract: Excitons in atomically thin semiconductors are almost always probed through their optical signatures, because the short lifetimes of these transient quasiparticles are generally assumed to preclude electrical detection. Here we show that photoexcited excitons in monolayer tungsten disulfide produce a large, optically tunable permittivity at gigahertz frequencies, and that the effect provides a contact-free electrical route to imaging dark excitons at the nanoscale. Using laser-illuminated microwave impedance microscopy, we find that high-purity encapsulated flakes exhibit a purely dielectric response resonant with the exciton spectrum, whereas defect-rich samples are governed by conventional photoconductivity. Spatial mapping of diffusion and sublinear power dependence identify long-lived dark excitons as the dominant contributors, and first-principles modelling of exciton polarizability reproduces the measured susceptibility. Our results establish excitons as optically tunable dielectric elements and introduce microwave microscopy as a direct electrical probe of dark-exciton transport with sub-100 nm resolution.

</details>


### [116] [Non-Hermitian Topology from Edge Transport in Hermitian Quantum Anomalous Hall Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23099)
*Humian Zhou,Ming Lu,Chui-Zhen Chen,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文证明全局厄米的量子反常霍尔系统可自然地产生内禀非厄米输运响应：单向手征边缘模与扩散型平庸边缘模的相互作用导致连续Hatano–Nelson模型所描述的固有非互易输运，非厄米趋肤效应直接编码于霍尔条可观测量的手征依赖指数空间分布中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米物理（如奇异点、非厄米趋肤效应）以往主要在依赖受控增益与损耗的工程化系统中实现，缺乏在天然厄米固态系统中自发涌现的机制。作者旨在证明非厄米输运响应可作为全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统的内禀性质出现，无需外部非厄米工程。

Method: 作者分析单向手征边缘模与扩散型平庸边缘模之间的相互作用，将其映射为连续Hatano–Nelson模型以刻画非互易输运；并采用Landauer–Büttiker输运模拟验证所预测的输运特征，同时论证磁性拓扑绝缘体是可行的实现平台。

Result: 发现电化学势与局部热耗散呈现手征依赖的指数空间分布（即非厄米趋肤效应），纵向电导随系统尺寸指数衰减，而霍尔电导保持量子化；Landauer–Büttiker模拟确认了上述输运特征，并指认磁性拓扑绝缘体为现实可行平台。

Conclusion: 该工作将非厄米拓扑与介观输运相连接，表明非厄米趋肤效应可在纯厄米量子反常霍尔系统中内禀涌现并能通过霍尔条测量直接观测，为固态系统中的非厄米拓扑器件开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian physics, known for exotic phenomena like exceptional points and the skin effect, has been most prominently realized in engineered systems relying on controlled gain and loss. Here we show that it can also arise naturally as an intrinsic transport response of a globally Hermitian quantum anomalous Hall system, without the need for external non-Hermitian engineering. We show that the interplay between unidirectional chiral edge modes and diffusive normal edge modes induces intrinsic non-reciprocal transport described by a continuum Hatano-Nelson model. Consequently, the non-Hermitian skin effect is encoded directly in experimentally accessible Hall-bar observables: the electrochemical potential and local heat dissipation acquire chirality-dependent exponential spatial profiles, while the longitudinal conductance decays exponentially with system size and the Hall conductance remains quantized. Using Landauer--B{"u}ttiker simulations, we confirm these transport signatures and identify magnetic topological insulators as a realistic platform for an intrinsic non-Hermitian transport response. Our results bridge non-Hermitian topology with mesoscopic transport, opening a pathway toward non-Hermitian topological devices in solid-state systems.

</details>


### [117] [Magnetic Quadrupole Lens and Current-Induced Quadrupolar Dynamics of Antiskyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23129)
*Linfeng Du,Yang Liu,Peng Yan,Ying Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 反斯格明子可作为磁四极透镜，对穿越其中的电流进行聚焦或散焦，揭示出高度各向异性的四极反斯格明子霍尔效应，可提升反斯格明子赛道存储性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反斯格明子赛道存储面临电流驱动下霍尔角过大导致纹理偏离轨道的难题，亟需理解并调控电流与反斯格明子的耦合动力学，而反斯格明子对电流分布的反作用此前未被充分认识。

Method: 将量子输运计算与微磁学模拟迭代耦合，自洽求解传导电子与反斯格明子的相互作用；分析不同注入方向下反斯格明子核作为焦点的电流汇聚行为及其经自旋转移矩引起的动力学改变。

Result: 反斯格明子核在特定注入方向成为电流焦点，产生各向异性且非均匀的电流分布；出现对电流方向高度各向异性、并可被自旋轨道耦合有效调控的四极反斯格明子霍尔效应。电流聚焦显著提高反斯格明子速度并降低其霍尔角，散焦则相反。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了本征电流透镜效应作为操控拓扑磁结构的有效机制，可克服反斯格明子赛道存储的关键瓶颈，并为反斯格明子自旋电子器件开辟新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that antiskyrmions can serve as magnetic quadrupole lenses that focus or defocus electric currents traversing them. We showcase the antiskyrmion core as a focal point where electron beams converge for specific injection directions, leading to anisotropic and nonuniform current distributions. Thus the current-induced antiskyrmion dynamics is profoundly modified by the magnetic quadrupole lensing via the spin-transfer torque. By incorporating quantum transport and micromagnetic simulations iteratively, we self-consistently capture the coupled dynamics of conduction electrons and antiskyrmions. Our results unveil a quadrupolar antiskyrmion Hall effect that is highly anisotropic with respect to the current direction and can be effectively tuned by the spin-orbit coupling. In particular, the current focusing (defocusing) dramatically enhances (suppresses) the antiskyrmion speed while simultaneously reducing its Hall angle, thereby overcoming a key challenge for antiskyrmion racetrack memories. Our work establishes the intrinsic current lensing as a powerful mechanism for manipulating topological magnetic textures and opens new avenues for antiskyrmion-based spintronic devices.

</details>


### [118] [Collective depinning of dipolar skyrmion chains: thermal and athermal rounding of an elastic polymer in a quenched landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23181)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过随机Thiele方程数值求解，研究弱钉扎势中偶极斯格明子链的退钉扎动力学，揭示阈值电流与链长的无关性、热致转变展宽及磁场对转变形状的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯格明子在基于该结构的器件中常以链而非孤立织构的形式作为信息载体，因此需理解相互作用斯格明子链在钉扎势中的集体退钉扎行为，而非仅关注单个斯格明子。

Method: 采用随机Thiele方程数值求解，模拟不同长度、周期性边界条件下的偶极斯格明子链在淬火吸引钉扎位点上的驱动，并在不同磁场下测量退钉扎阈值、转变宽度及内禀Larkin关联长度。

Result: (i) 长度≥50的链退钉扎阈值与链长无关，约为孤立斯格明子的三分之一；(ii)转变展宽仅部分源于热效应，T=0时宽度0.334±0.004 decade，300 K时增至0.633±0.002，而Larkin关联长度保持约2.6键；(iii)宽度在kBT~0.14V0前几乎不变，到kBT~0.23V0增大近四倍，主要来自蠕移区展宽，且与链长无关；磁场从30 mT升至40 mT使转变核心展宽26%并缩短蠕移尾部。约200个斯格明子的链通过内部形变序列退钉扎：Larkin长度与粗糙度增长、瞬态键拉伸、随后迁移率与霍尔角恢复。

Conclusion: 斯格明子链的退钉扎由集体效应主导，阈值不受有限尺寸影响，转变展宽兼具热与非热成分，并可通过磁场调控形状；这些结果对以链为信息载体的斯格明子器件设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: The dynamics of interacting dipole skyrmion chains in a weak pinning landscape is studied through numerical solutions of the stochastic Thiele equation. Chains of different lengths with periodic boundary conditions are driven across quenched attractive pinning sites under different magnetic fields. A clear threshold spin current is identified as the critical depinning force. Three main results emerge: (i) for chains of 50 skyrmions or more, the depinning threshold is independent of chain length and remains close to one third of that of an isolated skyrmion. (ii) The transition rounding is only partly thermal: the width changes from w = 0.334 +/- 0.004 decades at T = 0 to 0.633 +/- 0.002 at 300 K, while the intrinsic Larkin correlation length remains about 2.6 bonds below kBT ~ V0/25. (iii) The width is nearly unchanged up to kBT ~ 0.14 V0, but increases almost fourfold by kBT ~ 0.23 V0, mainly through broadening of the creep regime. It is independent of chain length, ruling out finite-size rounding. Increasing the field from 30 to 40 mT changes the transition shape, widening its core by 26% while shortening the creep tails. For chains of about 200 skyrmions, depinning proceeds through a sequence of internal deformations - growth of the Larkin length and roughness, transient bond stretching, and subsequent recovery of mobility and Hall angle. These collective effects are relevant for skyrmion-based devices in which chains, rather than isolated textures, act as information carriers.

</details>


### [119] [Non-Markovian Quantum Dynamics of Exciton-Polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23295)
*Rajanya Sarkar,Pritha Ghosh,Arshath Manjalingal,David R. Reichman,Arkajit Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种非马尔可夫主方程方法，结合波矢空间粗粒化与密度矩阵对角元并行演化策略，实现了激子-极化激元非平衡动力学的全量子模拟，并在TIPS-并五苯中再现了实验观测到的极化激元群速度重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子-极化激元在室温下表现出鲁棒的介观相干量子输运，但精确且不依赖半经典近似的量子动力学模拟计算成本极高，难以从微观层面理解声子、光子与电子在集体光-物质耦合下的复杂动力学相互作用。

Method: 发展非马尔可夫主方程方法，超越传统马尔可夫极限以刻画声子诱导的退相干与耗散；同时提出将波矢空间粗粒化、并对密度矩阵各对角元并行演化的计算流程，从而实现全量子力学模拟。

Result: 在TIPS-并五苯中模拟激子-极化激元输运，合理地再现了实验观测的极化激元群速度重整化，并表明该重整化源于声子诱导的非马尔可夫Lamb位移；进一步证明传统马尔可夫理论无法复现该效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的非马尔可夫主方程框架可在可承受计算成本下进行全量子激子-极化激元动力学模拟，揭示非马尔可夫效应对极化激元输运的关键作用，为微观理解集体光-物质耦合下的多体动力学提供有效工具。

Abstract: Exciton-polaritons, hybrid light-matter quasiparticles formed when a material interacts with a confined electric field, have experimentally been shown to exhibit mesoscale coherent quantum propagation that remains robust at room temperature. However, an accurate and direct quantum dynamical simulation of this phenomenon that does not resort to semi-classical approximations is prohibitively expensive computationally, limiting the microscopic understanding of the rich dynamical interplay among phonons, photons, and electrons under collective light-matter coupling. To address this fundamental challenge, we develop a non-Markovian master equation approach which enables the fully quantum mechanical simulation of non-equilibrium exciton-polariton dynamics and captures phonon-induced decoherence and dissipation beyond the conventional Markovian limit. To carry out this task, a procedure is developed in which the wave vector space is coarse-grained and each diagonal element of the density matrix is evolved in parallel. To demonstrate the utility of this approach, we simulate exciton-polariton transport in TIPS-pentacene. We find that our approach reasonably captures the experimentally observed renormalization of the polariton group velocity, which originates from the phonon-induced non-Markovian Lamb shift. We further show that this renormalization cannot be reproduced within conventional Markovian theories.

</details>


### [120] [Non-Hermitian Quantum Mechanics I: Instantaneous Self-Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23426)
*Lingfeng Liu,Wei-Wei Yang,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文从闭合量子系统的幺正演化出发，严格证明全局波函数在任意局域子系统上的投影满足精确的含时非厄米薛定谔方程，其核心是含时非厄米的瞬时自能，无需任何常规近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效非厄米动力学在量子系统中广泛出现，但通常依赖Born近似、Markov近似、宽频带极限等常规近似，缺乏严格的微观基础，且这些近似的适用范围与误差界限不清晰。

Method: 从闭合量子系统的幺正演化出发进行严格推导，将全局波函数投影到任意局域子系统，引入含时且一般非厄米的"瞬时自能"（instantaneous self-energy）作为环境反作用项，与子系统哈密顿量共同构成精确的时间局域生成元；并将常规非厄米近似与该精确框架进行基准对比。

Result: 推导出不依赖Born近似、Markov近似或宽频带极限的精确含时非厄米薛定谔方程；通过基准对比系统性地揭示了常规非厄米近似的局限性。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子系统中有效非厄米动力学的涌现提供了严格的微观基础，明确了常规非厄米近似的适用边界。

Abstract: Starting from the unitary evolution of a closed quantum system, we rigorously demonstrate that the projection of the global wavefunction onto an arbitrary local subsystem is governed by an exact, time-dependent non-Hermitian Schrödinger equation. Crucially, the derivation does not rely on conventional approximations such as the Born approximation, the Markov approximation, or the wide-band limit. The central quantity is the \textit{instantaneous self-energy}, a time-dependent and generally non-Hermitian operator that encodes environmental backaction and, together with the subsystem Hamiltonian, forms the exact time-local generator of the projected dynamics. By benchmarking the conventional non-Hermitian approximation against this exact framework, we systematically expose its limitations. These results provide a rigorous microscopic foundation for the emergence of effective non-Hermitian dynamics in quantum systems.

</details>


### [121] [Sub-Thermionic Switching in a Negative-Capacitance Bandgap-Change Field-Effect Transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23505)
*Rahul B Awale,Mike Klymenko,Yuefeng Yin,Nikhil V Medhekar,Bhaskaran Muralidharan,Michael S Fuhrer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出并建模了负电容带隙变化场效应晶体管（NC-BCFET），用铁电栅堆叠放大打开双层石墨烯带隙的电场，在300 K实现15 mV/dec的亚阈值摆幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破亚阈值摆幅约60 mV/dec的玻尔兹曼极限是低功耗纳电子学的核心挑战。负电容场效应晶体管通过放大沟道电势来应对，但其有效性受电容匹配和传统沟道量子电容的限制。

Method: 构建NC-BCFET模型：铁电栅堆叠放大打开沟道带隙的电场，采用双层石墨烯（电可调带隙沟道）与Al0.55Sc0.45N（负电容铁电体）等实验已验证材料。自洽框架耦合四带紧束缚哈密顿量与GW修正屏蔽、准静态Landau–Devonshire铁电响应及弹道Landauer–Büttiker输运。

Result: 负电容放大层间电势差，使开关窗口相对介电栅控器件压缩37倍。300 K时优化NC-BCFET的亚阈值摆幅为15 mV/dec，低于玻尔兹曼极限四倍，100 K时提升至2.5 mV/dec。室温开关比受双层石墨烯带隙限制约为10^2。

Conclusion: 该概念可直接推广至具有更大场可调带隙的材料，从而在室温下同时实现深度亚热离子开关与高开关比。

Abstract: Overcoming the approximately 60~mV/dec Boltzmann limit of the subthreshold swing remains a central challenge for low-power nanoelectronics. Negative-capacitance field-effect transistors address it by amplifying the channel potential, but their effectiveness is constrained by capacitance matching and by the quantum capacitance of conventional channels. Here we model a negative-capacitance bandgap-change field-effect transistor (NC-BCFET), in which the ferroelectric gate stack instead amplifies the electric field that opens the bandgap of the channel, using experimentally proven materials: bilayer graphene as a channel with an electrically tunable bandgap and Al$_{0.55}$Sc$_{0.45}$N as the negative-capacitance ferroelectric. Our self-consistent framework couples a four-band tight-binding Hamiltonian with GW-corrected screening, a quasistatic Landau--Devonshire ferroelectric response, and ballistic Landauer--Büttiker transport. Negative capacitance amplifies the interlayer potential difference, compressing the switching window 37-fold relative to a dielectric-gated control. At 300~K, the optimized NC-BCFET achieves a subthreshold swing of 15~mV/dec, four times below the Boltzmann limit, improving to 2.5~mV/dec at 100~K. The room-temperature on/off ratio is limited to $\sim 10^{2}$ by the bandgap of bilayer graphene. The concept extends directly to materials with larger field-tunable bandgaps, where deep subthermionic switching and high on/off ratios can be achieved together at room temperature.

</details>


### [122] [Universal Generalized Brillouin Zone Theory I: Review of the Spectral Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23523)
*Zeqi xu,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本系列论文旨在建立高维非厄米系统的通用广义布里渊区（GBZ）理论。本文作为开篇，聚焦一维GBZ条件向二维过渡时失效的机制，并指出既有1D GBZ理论本身的不完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维GBZ条件 |β_p|=|β_{p+1}| 在二维失效，但其失效的机制——即系统从1D逐步过渡到2D时如何发生——尚不清楚。探究此问题发现既有1D GBZ理论本身仍不完备，这构成了本文的出发点。

Method: 系统梳理一维谱方法（spectral approach），厘清其内在困难所在，并识别推动后续波函数方法（wavefunction approach）发展的关键挑战。

Result: 明确了现有1D GBZ谱方法的关键困难与挑战，为论文II中提出的波函数方法奠定动机与基础。

Conclusion: 1D GBZ理论尚不完整；需发展新方法，本文通过指出谱方法的困难，导出后续波函数方法的研究路线。

Abstract: This series of papers aims to establish a universal generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) theory for higher-dimensional non-Hermitian systems. As the starting point of this series, we emphasize a fundamental question: while the conventional one-dimensional (1D) GBZ condition, $|β_p| = |β_{p+1}|$, is well known to fail in two dimensions, how does this breakdown actually occur as a system gradually crosses over from 1D to 2D? Investigating this question reveals that the existing 1D GBZ theory itself remains incomplete. In this first paper, we therefore systematically review the 1D spectral approach and clarify where the underlying difficulties lie. Our work identifies the key challenges that motivate the wavefunction approach developed in Paper~II.

</details>


### [123] [Multiscale Modeling of Ion Transport in Nanopores: Fitting Implicit-Water Radial Diffusion Profiles to Explicit-Water Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23575)
*Mónika Valiskó,Salman Shabbir,Eszter Molnárné Lakics,Zoltán Ható,Dezső Boda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多尺度方法：将显式水分子动力学（MD）模拟得到的径向电导率信息，通过拟合径向变化的有效扩散系数，纳入Nernst-Planck + 局域平衡蒙特卡洛（NP+LEMC）框架，用于模拟纳米孔内离子输运，成功复现MD的径向电流分布及NaCl、CaCl$_2$及其混合物的差异化输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔离子输运的精确模拟存在尺度矛盾：显式水MD能提供分子尺度的输运信息，但计算代价高、难以用于器件尺度的实验相关行为预测；而计算高效的连续介质模型（如Nernst-Planck）通常使用空间均匀的扩散系数，无法刻画孔壁附近因水结构、离子吸附导致的强迁移率抑制。因此需要一种桥接分子尺度信息与高效降阶模型的多尺度方案。

Method: 在NP+LEMC框架（Nernst-Planck方程耦合局域平衡蒙特卡洛，以计入超越平均场近似的离子关联）内，对显式水MD给出的径向电导率剖面拟合出径向变化的有效扩散系数剖面；将该有效扩散系数剖面应用于负电性二氧化硅纳米孔中NaCl、CaCl$_2$及其混合物的输运计算。

Result: 所得扩散系数剖面能够复现MD模拟的径向电流分布，包括孔壁附近离子迁移率的强烈抑制——这是孔内空间均匀扩散系数无法捕捉的。NaCl与CaCl$_2$呈现定性不同的输运行为：NaCl因近壁Na$^+$导电增强而表现为阳离子选择性；CaCl$_2$因强结合的Ca$^{2+}$在表面附近迁移率被强烈抑制而表现为弱阴离子选择性。NaCl--CaCl$_2$混合物中Ca$^{2+}$的优先结合导致离子电导的非线性变化。

Conclusion: 该方法在实验相关的器件行为与计算高效的降阶模型之间建立了桥梁：显式水MD提供分子尺度信息，并被纳入有效输运系数，从而在保持计算效率的同时保留关键的近壁分子尺度效应。

Abstract: We develop a multiscale approach for incorporating molecular-scale transport information into computationally efficient models of ion transport through nanopores. A radially varying effective diffusion coefficient profile is fitted to radial conductivity profiles obtained from explicit-water molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. The fitting is performed within the NP+LEMC framework, which combines the Nernst--Planck equation with Local Equilibrium Monte Carlo to account for ion correlations beyond mean-field approximation. We apply the approach to NaCl, CaCl$_2$, and their mixtures in a negatively charged silica nanopore. The resulting diffusion coefficient profiles reproduce the radial current distributions of the MD simulations, including the strong suppression of ionic mobility near the pore wall that cannot be captured by a spatially constant diffusion coefficient inside the pore. NaCl and CaCl$_2$ exhibit qualitatively different transport behavior: the former is cation selective due to enhanced near-wall Na$^+$ conduction, whereas the latter shows weak anion selectivity because strongly bound Ca$^{2+}$ ions have strongly suppressed mobility near the surface. For NaCl--CaCl$_2$ mixtures, preferential Ca$^{2+}$ binding leads to nonlinear changes in ionic conductance. The approach establishes a bridge between experimentally relevant device behavior and computationally efficient reduced models, with explicit-water MD providing the molecular-scale information that can be incorporated into the effective transport coefficients.

</details>


### [124] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling and magnetic chirality: Multidirectional spin polarization in a helical antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23618)
*Kallol Mondal,Sudin Ganguly,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在螺旋反铁磁体中，螺旋磁结构与非共线磁序的对称性破缺可在x、y、z三个方向同时产生自旋极化，且自旋轨道耦合与参数（尤其长程跃迁）显著调控该自旋过滤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋过滤系统通常只产生沿单一方向的自旋极化，本文旨在探究非共线螺旋反铁磁体结合自旋轨道耦合能否打破这一限制，实现多方向自旋极化。

Method: 采用紧束缚框架描述非共线螺旋反铁磁体，纳入自旋轨道耦合，并利用Landauer-Büttiker形式体系计算自旋相关的输运与自旋极化。

Result: 发现沿x、y、z三个方向可同时产生有限自旋极化，源于非共线磁序引起的对称性破缺；自旋轨道耦合显著改变自旋极化，且极化强烈依赖螺旋体系参数，长程跃迁明显增强自旋过滤效应，使三方向自旋极化分量均增大。

Conclusion: 非共线螺旋反铁磁体可实现多方向自旋极化，为超越传统单轴自旋过滤的自旋输运调控提供了新机制。

Abstract: We investigate spin-dependent transport in a non-collinear helical antiferromagnet in the presence of spin-orbit coupling within a tight-binding framework. Using the Landauer-Büttiker formalism, we analyze the generation of spin polarization arising from the combined effects of the helical magnetic texture and spin-orbit interaction. We find that finite spin polarization can be generated simultaneously along the $\hat{x}$, $\hat{y}$, and $\hat{z}$-directions, in contrast to the predominantly single-axis spin polarization commonly observed in conventional spin-filtering systems. The multidirectional spin polarization originates from the symmetry breaking introduced by the non-collinear magnetic order. We further show that the spin polarization is modified significantly in the presence of spin-orbit coupling and it has strong dependence on the parameters associated with the helical system. In particular, long-range hopping significantly enhances the spin-filtering effect, leading to large spin polarization components along all three spatial directions.

</details>


### [125] [Scalable Multiple Electron Transport Architectures for Feedback Traceable Current Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24015)
*Agustin Javier Lapi,Guillermo Fernandez Moroni,Fernando Chierchie,Fabricio Alcalde Bessia,Miqueas Ezequiel Gamero,Brenda Aurea Cervantes Vergara,Blas Junior Irigoyen Gimenez,Eduardo Paolini,Claudio Rodrigo Chavez Blanco,Juan Estrada,Javier Tiffenberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于浮栅架构的可扩展电子计数电流单元，作为多电子输运（MET）器件运行，实现离散电荷包的可控注入、传输与精确量化，并将电荷注入技术与电子分辨能力结合，用于生成可溯源至电子的量化电流源。实验验证了并联/串联多放大器架构下的噪声按独立测量数的平方根下降，并获得稳定可编程的输出电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现电子可溯源的量化电流源与精密量子计量，需要一种紧凑、可扩展且具备单电子分辨与低噪声能力的电子计数平台，以突破传统电流源在精度和可扩展性上的限制。

Method: 基于浮栅架构构建可扩展电子计数电流单元，将其作为多电子输运（MET）器件运行；利用传感器的非破坏性多次读出能力，配合复用的电荷注入技术，实现离散电荷包的可控注入、传输与精确量化，并探索并联与串联多放大器两种可扩展架构。

Result: 实验结果表明，噪声随独立测量数按平方根规律下降（符合统计平均预期），同时输出电流稳定且可编程。

Conclusion: 该方案为电子计数电流源与精密量子计量应用提供了一种紧凑、可扩展的平台，具备实现电子溯源量化电流源的潜力。

Abstract: We present a scalable electron-counting current cell based on a floating gate architecture operated as a Multiple Electron Transport device (MET). The system enables controlled injection, transport, and precise quantification of discrete charge packets using the sensor non-destructive multiple readout capability. We repurposed the charge-injection technique, jointly with the electron-resolution capability, for a controlled generation of quantized charge packets for an electron-traceable current source.
  Scalable architectures based on parallel and series multi-amplifier configurations are explored. Experimental results confirm noise reduction following the square root of the number of independent measurements and demonstrate stable, programmable output current. This approach provides a compact and scalable platform for electron-counting current sources and precision quantum metrology applications.

</details>


### [126] [The Superconducting Talbot Effect in Phased-Array Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24572)
*Hechen Ren,Ziying Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出超导Talbot效应这一宏观量子自成像现象，并通过相控阵约瑟夫森结与游标式收集器阵列实现对分数Talbot图样的亚波长采样与主动调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现超导近邻库珀对在弹道二维电子气中传播时的自成像，并克服输运测量分辨率限制，从而对量子干涉进行高鲁棒性的实空间成像与材料诊断。

Method: 将周期性超导电极构造成具有可编程相位差的相控阵约瑟夫森结，主动操控超导Talbot地毯；设计游标式收集器阵列进行分数Talbot图样的亚波长采样；在方格格子上开展紧束缚数值计算验证。

Result: 成功映射实空间量子干涉且对无序具有高鲁棒性，可直接提取helical、spin-degenerate与spin-orbit-split费米面的费米波长；紧束缚数值结果验证了实空间干涉图样。

Conclusion: 该工作为超导光学中的相干波前工程与量子材料诊断建立了通用框架。

Abstract: We introduce the superconducting Talbot effect---a macroscopic quantum self-imaging phenomenon occurring when proximitized Cooper pairs propagate through a ballistic two-dimensional electron gas. By configuring periodic superconducting leads into a phased-array Josephson junction with programmable phase differences, we demonstrate active steering of the resulting superconducting Talbot carpet. To overcome transport resolution limits, we design a Vernier-scale collector array that performs sub-wavelength sampling of the fractional Talbot pattern. This approach maps real-space quantum interference with high robustness to disorder, enabling direct extraction of Fermi wavelengths across helical, spin-degenerate, and spin-orbit-split Fermi surfaces. Tight-binding numerical calculations on a square lattice validate the real-space interference patterns. Our results establish a versatile framework for coherent wavefront engineering and quantum materials diagnostics in superconducting optics.

</details>


### [127] [Corbino-Enhanced Supersonic Acoustic-Emission Threshold in a GaAs Two-Dimensional Electron System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24585)
*A. D. Levin,A. S. Jaroshevich,Z. D. Kvon,V. A. Chitta,M. S. Aksenov,D. V. Dmitriev,A. K. Bakarov,G. M. Gusev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了高迁移率GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子系统（Corbino几何）中的非线性差分电阻测量，在零磁场下负极电流端观察到极性选择性的阈值峰，并将其解释为局域Cherenkov型声子发射阈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高迁移率电子系统中的局域非平衡电子-声子过程及其在流体力学交叉区中的表现缺乏敏感探测手段，作者希望通过Corbino几何的径向电流集中效应来探索电子速度超过声速的局域物理。

Method: 在Corbino几何的高迁移率GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子系统中进行非线性差分电阻测量，测量差分电阻随直流电流及垂直磁场的变化，分析径向电流密度j_r(r)=I/(2πr)导致的局域电子速度空间分布。

Result: 零磁场下差分电阻R_diff(I_dc)在负电流端出现显著极性选择性阈值峰，该峰随垂直磁场逐渐被抑制；极性选择性归因于Peltier加热与冷却对接触附近局域热边界条件的调制，而非运动学阈值本身。

Conclusion: Corbino几何可作为探测高迁移率电子系统中局域非平衡电子-声子过程的灵敏平台，且内Corbino接触可作为几何定义下的超声声发射源。

Abstract: We report nonlinear differential-resistance measurements in a high-mobility GaAs/AlGaAs two-dimensional electron system patterned in a Corbino geometry. At zero magnetic field, $R_{\mathrm{diff}}(I_{dc})$ exhibits a pronounced polarity-selective threshold peak on the negative-current branch, gradually suppressed by a perpendicular magnetic field. We interpret the Corbino anomaly as a local Cherenkov-like threshold for acoustic phonon emission, enabled by the radial current concentration $j_r(r)=I/(2πr)$, which drives the local electron velocity above the sound velocity in a region adjacent to the inner contact. The polarity selectivity is attributed to Peltier heating and cooling, which modify the local thermal and boundary conditions near the contact, rather than to the kinematic threshold itself. Our results identify the Corbino geometry as a sensitive platform for probing local nonequilibrium electron--phonon processes in a high-mobility electron system within an independently established hydrodynamic-crossover regime and suggest that the inner Corbino contact can act as a geometry-defined source for supersonic acoustic emission.

</details>


### [128] [Observation of Kondo Effect in Rhombohedral Graphene Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24734)
*Xin Liao,Qing Yin,Huiwen Wang,Jing-Wei Dong,Jun-Xi Chen,Si-Li Wu,Cai-Zhen Li,Guowei Lyu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wei Jiang,Yu-Gui Yao,Zhi-Min Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在六层菱方石墨烯莫尔超晶格中通过磁输运与变温测量实验观测到近藤效应，可用磁场或温度诱导的近藤单态断裂一致解释，并发现近藤相互作用可由载流子密度与位移电场连续调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联体系中的近藤效应源于巡游传导电子与局域磁矩之间的反铁磁耦合，可产生多种奇异量子现象。二维莫尔超晶格具有拓扑平带，其中Wannier轨道被周期性莫尔势空间局域化，可作为局域磁矩，从而为可观测的近藤效应提供高度可调的理想平台。

Method: 采用磁输运测量与温度依赖测量，对六层菱方石墨烯莫尔超晶格进行研究；通过改变磁场、温度、载流子密度n和位移电场D，系统考察磁电阻、霍尔电阻R_xy和纵向电阻R_xx的响应。

Result: 随磁场增大，磁电阻在临界场B_c处呈现先增后减的转变，霍尔电阻R_xy在B_c附近发生符号反转；随温度降低，纵向电阻R_xx先对数增长，随后按T^2行为下降，表明向重费米液体转变。这些行为可由磁场或温度诱导的近藤单态断裂一致解释：原本束缚于局域磁矩的载流子被释放，从而提高电导并改变主导载流子类型。此外，近藤相互作用可由载流子密度n与位移电场D连续调控，并暗示存在近藤绝缘态。

Conclusion: 研究结果深化了对莫尔工程平带体系中近藤效应的理解，为探索奇异物关联量子相提供了新途径。

Abstract: Kondo effect in strongly correlated systems arises from the antiferromagnetic coupling between itinerant conduction electrons and localized magnetic moments, giving rise to a variety of exotic quantum phenomena. Two-dimensional moiré superlattice systems provide a highly tunable platform featuring topological flat bands, where Wannier orbitals are spatially confined by the periodic moiré potential and serve as localized magnetic moments, enabling the observable Kondo effect. Here we experimentally demonstrate Kondo interactions in hexalayer rhombohedral graphene moiré superlattices through magneto-transport and temperature-dependent measurements. With increasing magnetic field, the magnetoresistance exhibits an increase-decrease transition across a critical field B_c, while the Hall resistance R_xy undergoes a sign reversal near B_c. Moreover, as temperature decreases, the longitudinal resistance R_xx first increases logarithmically and then decreases following a T^2 behavior, indicating a transition to heavy fermion liquid. These behaviors can be consistently explained by the breakdown of Kondo singlets induced by either magnetic field or temperature, which liberates carriers previously bound to localized moments, thereby enhancing conductivity and altering the dominant carrier type. Furthermore, our results demonstrate that the Kondo interaction can be continuously tuned by both carrier density n and displacement electric field D, and suggest the emergence of a Kondo insulating state. Our findings provide deep insight into the Kondo effect in moiré engineered flat-band systems, paving the path for exploring exotic correlated quantum phases.

</details>


### [129] [Modified Low-Temperature Scanning Tunneling Microscope for Ultrafast Pump-Probe Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24916)
*Vibhuti N. Rai,Junyoung Sim,Nils Bogdanoff,Sergey Trishin,Florian Faaber,Paul Wiechers,Caroline Firschke,Tobias Kampfrath,Christian Lotze,Katharina J. Franke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过对Besocke"Beetle"型STM进行最小改造，在维持低于6 K低温的同时实现了太赫兹（THz）脉冲与光脉冲向STM结的高效耦合，并用单层MoS₂/Au(111)的THz诱导隧穿电流成像验证了原子级分辨率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THz-STM可将声子、自旋和载流子超快动力学探测提升到原子级空间与亚皮秒时间分辨率，但将THz辐射耦合进低温STM结存在技术难题：既要为激光照明提供光学通路，又要保持结的稳定性。通常从头设计全新STM头部及定制光学通路与腔内光学系统代价高，因此需要一种低改动、低成本的改造方案。

Method: 在仅对低温辐射屏蔽罩做最小改动的前提下，改造Besocke"Beetle"型STM，使其在给定几何条件下仍能高效耦合THz与光脉冲并保持6 K以下温度。光学系统置于独立激光台上：以飞秒激光脉冲在LiNbO₃晶体中产生THz脉冲。通过互相关测量获得THz脉冲波形并确定有效时间分辨率。以Au(111)上生长的单层MoS₂为样品，测量THz诱导隧穿电流并进行电流成像。

Result: 获得了THz脉冲波形并确定了有效时间分辨率；成功测得单层MoS₂/Au(111)的THz诱导隧穿电流，电流图呈现原子级对比度并分辨出一个点缺陷，证实了THz耦合效率与低温STM在原子分辨率下的稳定运行。

Conclusion: 无需从头设计全新STM头部，仅对现有Besocke"Beetle"型STM的低温辐射屏蔽罩作最小改动，即可在6 K以下实现THz与光脉冲的高效耦合及稳定的原子级成像，为在常规低温STM上开展THz-STM超快动力学研究提供了可行且低成本的改造路径。

Abstract: Combining scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) with terahertz pulses (THz-STM) enables ultrafast dynamics of phonons, spins, and charge carriers to be probed with atomic-scale spatial and sub-picosecond temporal resolution. However, coupling THz radiation into a low-temperature STM junction presents some technical challenges as it requires optical access for laser illumination while maintaining junction stability. Instead of designing a completely new STM head with customized optical access and optics within the ultra-high vacuum chamber, we modified a Besocke "Beetle" style STM with minimal changes of the cryogenic radiation shields. Despite the given geometrical conditions, we achieved efficient coupling of THz and optical pulses to the STM junction while maintaining temperatures below 6 K. We designed the optical setup on a separate laser table, starting with femtosecond laser pulses that are used to generate THz pulses in a LiNbO$_3$ crystal. We show the obtained THz pulse shapes and determine the effective time resolution using cross-correlation measurements. We further demonstrate the stable performance of the modified STM by measuring THz-induced tunneling currents from monolayer molybdenum disulfide (MoS$_2$) grown on Au(111). The resulting current maps resolve atomic-scale contrast revealing a point defect. The measurements confirm efficient THz coupling and stable low-temperature STM operation with atomic resolution.

</details>


### [130] [Model of the magnon Kerr effect in a highly anisotropic ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24959)
*Davit Petrosyan,Hiroki Matsumoto,Hanchen Wang,Richard Schlitz,Pietro Gambardella,William Legrand*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有单轴各向异性薄膜的磁振子克尔效应哈密顿量，计算了外加磁场平行、垂直及成中间角时对应的克尔系数，并给出了非线性随磁振子频率与样品体积的标度关系，进而建立了混合腔-磁振子系统的运动方程以获取光子/磁振子布居及微波透射散射矩阵元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子克尔效应是任何具有有限磁各向异性铁磁体中普遍存在的非线性现象，但针对薄膜体系在不同外场取向下的系统性理论描述仍不完整，且缺乏与实验可观测的腔微波透射量的直接联系。

Method: 基于单轴各向异性薄膜推导磁振子克尔效应哈密顿量；分别考虑外磁场平行、垂直以及与各向异性轴成中间角三种构型，逐一确定克尔系数；分析非线性随磁振子频率和磁样品体积的标度规律；并建立含薄膜克尔非线性的混合腔-磁振子系统的运动方程。

Result: 得到了三种外场取向下对应的克尔系数表达式，给出了非线性强度随磁振子频率与样品体积的标度关系，并推导出混合腔-磁振子系统中光子与磁振子的布居数以及微波透射的散射矩阵元。

Conclusion: 该工作为薄膜磁振子克尔非线性提供了统一的理论框架，并建立了其与可实验观测的腔微波透射响应之间的联系，有助于指导相关实验观测与材料/几何参数设计。

Abstract: The magnon Kerr effect is a nonlinear phenomenon that occurs universally in any ferromagnet with finite magnetic anisotropy. It manifests itself as a magnon population-dependent change in the magnon dispersion relation. Here, we derive the Hamiltonian for the magnon Kerr effect for a thin film with uniaxial anisotropy. We consider the external field, parallel, perpendicular, and at an intermediate angle to the anisotropy axis, and determine the Kerr coefficient in each case. We show how the nonlinearity scales for different magnetic materials as a function of magnon frequency and magnetic sample volume. Moreover, we derive the equations of motion for a hybrid cavity-magnon system with such thin-film magnon Kerr nonlinearity, to obtain populations of photons and magnons, and scattering matrix terms for the microwave transmission of the cavity, relevant for experimental observations.

</details>


### [131] [Collective Coordinate Dynamics of Antiferromagnet: Gravity by Quantum Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24975)
*Kuangyin Deng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 反铁磁/亚铁磁织构的集体坐标动力学由Néel矢量CP¹态的量子几何决定，表现为测地线方程，产生有效黎曼引力与洛伦兹力，与铁磁中无惯性的Thiele方程本质不同；应用于光驱动超快反铁磁畴壁，解释实验观测的螺旋度依赖非对称运动并预言畴壁宽度振荡呼吸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁织构运动通常用Thiele方程描述，该方程无惯性、不存在引力类比，难以刻画反铁磁与亚铁磁织构的动力学行为，因此需要一个包含量子几何效应、能统一描述其惯性与规范场耦合的新理论框架。

Method: 将反铁磁和亚铁磁织构的集体坐标动力学从Néel矢量对应的CP¹态量子几何出发进行推导，把运动方程约化为由量子几何度量与联络控制的测地线方程，从而自然导出有效黎曼引力与洛伦兹力；并用该框架分析光驱动的超快反铁磁畴壁。

Result: 理论表明反铁磁/亚铁磁织构运动受有效黎曼引力与洛伦兹力支配，与铁磁的Thiele图像（无惯性、无引力类比）根本不同；应用于光学驱动的超快反铁磁畴壁，成功解释了实验中观测到的螺旋度依赖（helicity-dependent）非对称运动，并预言畴壁宽度会出现显著的呼吸式振荡。

Conclusion: 反铁磁与亚铁磁织构的动力学可统一为量子几何诱导的测地线运动，揭示了一种新的惯性、引力类比及规范场耦合机制，超越了传统Thiele方程；该框架不仅能解释已有超快畴壁实验，还给出畴壁宽度呼吸等可检验预言，为反铁磁自旋电子学与超快磁动力学提供新的理论工具。

Abstract: The collective coordinate dynamics of antiferromagnetic and ferrimagnetic textures emerges as a geodesic equation governed by the quantum geometry of the CP$^1$ state associated with the Néel vector, rendering an effective Riemannian gravity and a Lorentz force in the texture motion. This unveils a physical scenario fundamentally distinct from the Thiele equation widely used for ferromagnetic textures, which is inertialess without any gravity analogy. Applying our framework to an optically driven ultrafast antiferromagnetic domain wall, we can explain the helicity-dependent asymmetric motion observed experimentally and predict a vibrantly breathing wall width.

</details>


### [132] [Coherence protection of a silicon hole spin qubit with phase-modulated microwave driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19696)
*Sayyid I. Ibad,Yusuke Sato,Takuma Kuno,Itaru Yanagi,Toshiyuki Mine,Ryuta Tsuchiya,Digh Hisamoto,Hiroyuki Mizuno,Raisei Mizokuchi,Jun Yoneda,Tetsuo Kodera*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文针对硅量子点空穴自旋受电荷噪声与超精细噪声影响导致相干时间受限的问题，采用相位调制级联连续驱动（CCD）技术抑制低频噪声，稳定拉比振荡并延长衰减时间，实现了驱动过程中的相干保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅量子点空穴自旋因强自旋轨道耦合可实现快速全电控制，但该耦合也使其对电荷噪声更敏感；同时硅衬底残余核自旋的超精细相互作用引入低频噪声，二者共同限制相干时间。因此需要一种能抑制低频噪声、提升相干性的控制方案。

Method: 对空穴自旋量子比特实施相位调制的级联连续驱动（CCD）技术，通过微波相位调制来抑制低频噪声；并在CCD框架下定义量子比特，以在相干操控的同时实现对噪声的防护。

Result: 相比传统方法，该方案稳定了拉比振荡并延长了振荡衰减时间；在CCD框架下定义的量子比特可在门操作过程中保持相干性，证实了操控期间的相干保护效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方案是实现抗噪声空穴自旋量子比特的一条可行途径，为提升硅基空穴自旋量子比特的相干性提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Hole spins in silicon quantum dots are a promising platform for quantum computing due to their strong intrinsic spin-orbit coupling (SOC), which enables fast, all-electrical control. However, this coupling also increases their susceptibility to charge noise, thereby limiting coherence times. Moreover, holes in silicon are also affected by hyperfine interactions with residual nuclear spins in the silicon substrate, introducing a non-negligible source of low-frequency noise. Here, we implement a phase-modulated concatenated continuous driving (CCD) technique for hole spin qubits to suppress low-frequency noise through microwave phase modulation. This approach stabilizes Rabi oscillations and extends the oscillation decay time compared to the conventional method. Furthermore, by defining a qubit in the CCD frame, we achieve coherent control while simultaneously protecting the qubit from noise, confirming coherence protection during gate operations. These results demonstrate a viable route toward noise-robust hole spin qubits.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Universal Response Functions in Driven Dissipative Tunneling Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22328)
*Krishna Kingkar Pathak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究周期驱动与非局域耗散记忆同时作用下的势垒穿越过程，发现隧穿指数可分解为系统相关静态部分与一个两参数普适响应函数的乘积，从而确立了一类新的两参数普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性非平衡系统的普适性通常表现为标度律，使宏观行为对微观细节不敏感；但当周期驱动与非局域耗散记忆同时存在时，这种普适性是否仍然成立尚属未解问题。

Method: 采用包含 Floquet 调制与欧姆环境耦合的半经典瞬子框架，分析鞍点轨迹的修正；通过弱到中等耗散的解析展开处理有效作用量的动力学重整化，并对非局域瞬子作用量进行直接数值计算。

Result: 隧穿指数分解为系统相关的静态贡献与两个无量纲控制参数（归一化驱动频率与耗散强度）的普适函数；弱到中等耗散表现为光滑的动力学重整化并保持鞍点结构；高频区呈现普适动力学平均，且积分表示给出从绝热区到 Floquet 区的连续过渡；数值结果证实归一化隧穿指数在不同模型系统中具有普适依赖关系。

Conclusion: 驱动耗散势垒穿越构成非线性非平衡动力学中的一类独特两参数普适类，并为含记忆驱动系统的响应现象提供了可预测的函数框架。

Abstract: Universality in nonlinear nonequilibrium systems is typically expressed through scaling laws that render macroscopic behavior insensitive to microscopic details. Whether this universality survives when periodic forcing and nonlocal dissipative memory act simultaneously remains an open question in driven open dynamics. Here, we demonstrate that barrier-crossing processes in periodically driven dissipative systems are governed not merely by modified exponential scaling, but by an explicit two-parameter universal response function.
  Within a semiclassical instanton framework incorporating Floquet modulation and Ohmic environmental coupling, the tunneling exponent factorizes into a system-dependent static contribution and a universal function of two dimensionless control parameters: normalized driving frequency and dissipation strength. This factorization arises from the combined modification of a single saddle-point trajectory and introduces no additional independent scaling variables.
  Weak-to-moderate dissipation acts as a smooth dynamical renormalization of the effective action, preserving the saddle-point structure and enabling controlled analytical expansion. In the high-frequency regime, the response exhibits universal dynamical averaging, while an explicit integral representation establishes a continuous adiabatic to Floquet crossover. Direct numerical evaluation of the nonlocal instanton action confirms that the normalized tunneling exponent exhibits a universal dependence on the driving parameters across different model systems. These results identify driven dissipative barrier crossing as a distinct two-parameter universality class within nonlinear nonequilibrium dynamics and provide a predictive functional framework for response phenomena in driven systems with memory.

</details>
