### [162] [Bootstrap certification of string order in quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22420)
*Sagnik Banerjee,Haoyu Guo,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多体自举（bootstrap）框架，将弦算符视为基本对象，利用其与局域词构成的封闭算符代数，把弦关联的计算成本从随算符尺寸指数增长降为随弦长多项式增长，从而无需波函数即可严格认证拓扑相中的非局域弦序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体自举通过仅施加正定性约束、在关联子上最小化能量来认证基态性质，无需变分波函数。但其成本随所涉算符尺寸指数增长，使得诊断拓扑相所需的长程弦关联算符无法处理。作者希望突破这一指数瓶颈，实现非局域序的无波函数认证。

Method: 将弦算符作为主要对象处理：裸弦与端点修饰弦彼此之间以及与局域词之间满足封闭的算符代数，由此构造一个半正定规划（SDP），其成本仅随弦长多项式增长。单次计算即可给出所有长度的弦关联估计；当基态能量被限定在一个窗口内时，可对每条目标弦给出严格的双侧界。方法在团簇Ising链与自旋-1 Heisenberg链上与DMRG进行基准对比。

Result: 在团簇Ising链和自旋-1 Heisenberg链上，该方法直接从哈密顿量出发认证了团簇相的非局域弦序与Haldane相的弦序，并与DMRG结果一致。严格认证的弦序参数显示，界的紧致程度由参与计算的算符种类决定，而非仅由弦长决定。

Conclusion: 通过利用弦与局域词的代数封闭性，该框架将空间延展的可观测量转化为多项式可处理的自举变量，为量子多体系统中非局域序的无波函数认证开辟了途径。

Abstract: The many-body bootstrap certifies ground-state properties by minimizing energy over correlators constrained only by positivity, without any variational wavefunction. Its cost, however, grows exponentially with the size of the operators involved, placing the long-range string correlators that diagnose topological phases out of reach. We overcome this by treating string operators as primary objects: bare and endpoint-dressed strings satisfy a closed operator algebra with one another and with local words, yielding a semidefinite program whose cost grows only polynomially with string length. A single computation then yields estimates of string correlators of all lengths. Rigorous two-sided bounds are obtained for each target string once the ground-state energy is pinned within a window. When benchmarked against density-matrix renormalization group computations on the cluster Ising and spin$-1$ Heisenberg chains, the method certifies the nonlocal string order of the cluster and Haldane phases directly from the Hamiltonian. Our rigorous certification of string order parameters reveal that the sharpness of the bounds is set by which operators enter the calculation, not by their length alone. By exploiting algebraic closure of strings and local words, our framework lifts spatially extended observables into polynomially tractable bootstrap variables, opening up a route to wavefunction-free certification of nonlocal order in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [163] [Approaching the Inverse Neutron Scattering Problem with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22535)
*Jingyi Luo,Fletcher Williams,Ang-Kun Wu,David Dahlbom,Cristian D. Batista,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明神经网络可高效求解量子磁体哈密顿量反问题：以蜂窝晶格Heisenberg模型的八参数族为基准，用合成中子散射谱训练FCNN、CNN1D、CNN2D，均能高精度反演参数，在10%噪声下仍保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非弹性中子散射（INS）测量数据确定量子磁体的微观哈密顿量是凝聚态物理的核心难题。动力学自旋结构因子原则上包含相互作用信息，但因参数空间庞大且实验噪声存在，从谱中提取哈密顿量参数属于高度非平凡的反问题。

Method: 以完全极化相下蜂窝晶格Heisenberg自旋模型的八参数族为基准，在线性自旋波理论框架内精确计算动力学自旋结构因子，构建大规模合成中子散射谱数据集；训练三种神经网络（全连接网络FCNN、一维卷积网络CNN1D、二维卷积网络CNN2D）直接从动力学响应推断哈密顿量参数，并测试其在随机噪声（幅度达信号强度的10%）下的稳健性。

Result: 三种网络架构均取得高预测精度，能够直接从谱中准确反演哈密顿量参数；在输入谱被幅度达信号强度10%的随机噪声污染时，训练模型仍保持稳健，可靠恢复参数。

Conclusion: 机器学习可作为从中子散射数据进行定量哈密顿量重构的有力框架，并为量子磁性材料的自动化表征提供实用途径。

Abstract: Determining the microscopic Hamiltonian of a quantum magnet from inelastic neutron scattering measurements remains a central challenge in condensed matter physics. While the dynamical spin structure factor contains, in principle, sufficient information about the underlying interactions, extracting Hamiltonian parameters from experimental spectra constitutes a highly nontrivial inverse problem due to the large parameter space and the presence of experimental noise. Here we demonstrate that neural networks can efficiently solve this inverse problem for a broad class of quantum magnets. As a benchmark, we consider an eight-parameter family of honeycomb lattice Heisenberg spin models in the fully polarized phase, where the dynamical spin structure factor can be computed exactly within linear spin-wave theory. Using a large synthetic dataset of neutron scattering spectra, we train neural networks to infer the underlying Hamiltonian parameters directly from the dynamical response. We compare the performance of three different architectures---fully connected neural networks (FCNNs), one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (CNN1Ds), and two-dimensional convolutional neural networks (CNN2Ds)---and find that all achieve high predictive accuracy. Remarkably, the trained models remain robust in the presence of substantial experimental uncertainty, reliably recovering the Hamiltonian parameters even when the input spectra are contaminated by random noise with amplitudes reaching $10\%$ of the signal intensity. Our results establish machine learning as a powerful framework for quantitative Hamiltonian reconstruction from neutron scattering data and provide a practical route toward automated characterization of quantum magnetic materials.

</details>


### [164] [Structural and magnetic properties of TbCuAs$_2$ studied by X-ray and neutron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22589)
*M. G. Kim,T. Heitmann,S. Boney,C. Neupane,R. Acevedo-Esteves,A. Sapkota,D. Evans,P. C. Canfield,C. Nelson,E. D. Mun,J. -W. Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过中子衍射、X射线共振磁散射和单晶X射线衍射，确定了TbCuAs₂中Tb磁矩方向与反铁磁排列方式，并揭示其结构与磁性的关联及温度依赖性，指出稀土铜砷化物家族低温电阻异常的共性物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TbCuAs₂在低温下出现反常的电阻率上升，被认为源于其磁基态，但其反铁磁有序的具体性质此前尚未确定，需要实验澄清。

Method: 结合单晶中子衍射、X射线共振磁散射和单晶X射线衍射三种手段，对TbCuAs₂进行综合测量，并与同族化合物（R = Sm、Gd）的已有研究进行对比。

Result: 明确确定了Tb磁矩的方向、沿高对称方向的反铁磁排列方式、结构与磁性之间的相互作用及其温度依赖性。

Conclusion: 稀土铜砷化物家族中表现出低温电阻异常的化合物，其结构与磁性质可能受共同的潜在物理机制支配。

Abstract: We investigated antiferromagnetic metal TbCuAs$_2$, which exhibits an anomalous resistivity upturn at low temperatures. This resistivity upturn has been proposed to originate from the material's magnetic ground state; however, the precise nature of the antiferromagnetic ordering in this compound has not been previously established. Here, we present a combined single crystal neutron diffraction, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering, and single crystal X-ray diffraction study of TbCuAs$_2$. These measurements unambiguously determine the Tb magnetic moment direction, the antiferromagnetic arrangement along high symmetry directions, the interplay between structure and magnetism, and their temperature dependence. Our observations are compared with previous studies on sister compounds in the rare-earth copper arsenide family ($R$CuAs$_2$, $R$ = Sm and Gd), which also displays resistivity anomalies. We suggest a common underlying physics governing the structural and magentic properties of rare-earth copper arsenides that exhibit low-temperature resistivity anomalies.

</details>


### [165] [Probing $d$-wave pairing in the $t$-$t'$-$U$ Hubbard model with tensor-backflow wave functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22618)
*Xiao Liang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于张量背流方法研究二维t-t'-U Hubbard模型的d波配对，能量与最先进神经量子态相当，t'=-0.2t增强d波配对涨落但未表现出ODLRO所需的广延标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近期张量背流方法准确描述Fermi-Hubbard模型能量与条纹态的基础上，探究t-t'-U Hubbard模型中d波配对行为，特别是已被报道具有增强d波配对的参数区间（n=0.875, U=8, t'=-0.2t）。

Method: 采用张量背流波函数：8×8格点用对称性投影加一步Lanczos改进，12×12和16×16格点仅用一步Lanczos。研究填充n=0.875、在位排斥U=8、次近邻跃迁t'=-0.2t的参数区间，通过实空间配对关联和配对密度矩阵谱权分析d波配对。

Result: 16×16周期边界下，未显式对称约束的一步Lanczos后相对能量差为4.4×10⁻³，与对称保持的神经量子态结果相当。t'=-0.2t时观察到更强的d波配对关联及水平与垂直键对分量间的特征相对符号。有限负t'相比t'=0增大了配对密度矩阵谱权，表明整体d波配对涨落增强。8×8格点在有无对称投影下领头本征值均增强；12×12和16×16的领头谱权分散于若干近简并本征模而非集中于单一主导模。

Conclusion: 张量背流方法在能量精度上可与最先进神经量子态竞争。t'=-0.2t增强并使d波配对涨落分布更广，但无单一本征值表现出ODLRO所需的广延标度，说明未观测到长程d波超导序。

Abstract: Building on the recently developed tensor-backflow method, which accurately describes energies and stripe states in the Fermi-Hubbard model, we investigate $d$-wave pairing in the two-dimensional $t$-$t'$-$U$ Hubbard model. For the $8\times8$ lattice, we improve the wave function using symmetry projections followed by one Lanczos step, whereas for the $12\times 12$ and $16\times 16$ lattices, we apply one Lanczos step without symmetry projection. We focus primarily on the parameter regime with filling $n=0.875$, on-site repulsion $U=8$ and next-nearest-neighbor hopping $t'=-0.2t$, a regime in which enhanced $d$-wave pairing has been reported. The resulting energies are competitive with those obtained using state-of-the-art neural quantum states. For example, on a $16\times 16$ lattice with periodic boundary conditions, the tensor-backflow wave function after one Lanczos step without explicit symmetry enforcement achieves a relative energy difference of $4.4\times 10^{-3}$ from the symmetry-preserving neural quantum state result. From the real space pair correlations, for $t'=-0.2t$, we observe stronger $d$-wave pair correlations together with the characteristic relative sign between horizontal and vertical bond-pair components. Compared with $t'=0$, finite negative $t'$ increases the spectral weight of the corresponding pair-density matrix, which indicates the enhanced overall $d$-wave pair fluctuations. The leading eigenvalue is enhanced on the $8\times8$ lattice for both with and without symmetry projections. For larger lattices such as $12\times 12$ and $16\times 16$, the leading pair-density matrix spectral weight is distributed among several nearly degenerate eigenmodes rather than being concentrated in a single dominant mode. This indicates enhanced and more broadly distributed $d$-wave pair fluctuations, but no single eigenvalue exhibits the extensive scaling required for ODLRO.

</details>


### [166] [On the precise boundary of the Néel antiferromagnetic phase in the Shastry-Sutherland model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22907)
*Rongyi Lv,Xiangjian Qian,Mingpu Qin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模DMRG计算确定Shastry-Sutherland模型中Néel反铁磁相边界，发现交错磁化在争议区间0.78≲J1/J2≤0.81内仍保持有限，从而支持PVBS与Néel AFM之间的直接相变，否定了中间量子自旋液相的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shastry-Sutherland模型的基态相图仍存在争议，尤其是PVBS相与Néel AFM相之间相变的性质尚在争论中：部分研究认为两相直接相变，另一些研究则提出两者之间存在一个狭窄的中间量子自旋液相。因此需要精确确定Néel AFM相边界以厘清该争议。

Method: 采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算，通过施加交错边界钉扎场并直接测量体交错磁化，结合针对截断误差的系统外推以及细致的有限尺寸标度分析来确定Néel AFM相边界。

Result: 交错磁化在争议区间0.78≲J1/J2≤0.81内始终保持有限，而此前已达成共识的PVBS相边界位于J1/J2≈0.78。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了反对中间量子自旋液相存在的证据，支持Shastry-Sutherland模型中PVBS与Néel AFM之间的直接相变。

Abstract: The ground state phase diagram of the Shastry-Sutherland model remains controversial. In particular, the nature of the transition between the plaquette valence bond solid (PVBS) phase and the Néel antiferromagnetic (AFM) phase is still under debate. While some studies suggest a direct transition between the two phases, others propose the existence of a narrow intermediate quantum spin liquid regime. To address this issue, we perform large-scale Density Matrix Renormalization Group calculations to determine the Néel AFM phase boundary in the Shastry-Sutherland model. By applying staggered boundary pinning fields and directly measuring the bulk staggered magnetization, and through systematic extrapolations with respect to truncation error as well as careful finite-size scaling, we find that the staggered magnetization remains finite across the controversial region $0.78 \lesssim J_{1}/J_{2} \leq 0.81$, which was suggested to host a quantum spin liquid phase in some studies. Together with the previously established consensus placing the PVBS phase boundary at $J_{1}/J_{2} \approx 0.78$, these results provide evidence against the existence of an intermediate quantum spin liquid phase and support a direct PVBS--Néel AFM transition in the Shastry-Sutherland model.

</details>


### [167] [Correlation-assisted spin-selective metallicity in strained and bilayer altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23150)
*Marnin J. Di Nunzio,Frank Lechermann,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在单体与双层Hubbard型模型中，通过Hartree-Fock与RISB方法比较，揭示电子关联、掺杂、单轴应变与层间耦合如何调控交错磁序，证明交错磁序在准粒子重整化下保持稳健，并可实现完全自旋极化的费米面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体兼具零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂能带结构，可在无铁磁性的情况下提供自旋极化载流子。然而，电子关联及其他可调参量如何影响交错磁序及其输运性质尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建单体与双层系统的最小Hubbard型模型，采用Hartree-Fock理论与旋转不变Slave-Boson（RISB）方法进行对比计算，考察电子关联强度、掺杂、单轴应变与层间堆叠对交错磁序的调控。

Result: 交错磁序对准粒子重整化具有鲁棒性；磁序出现与准粒子相干性丧失在强关联下发生分离。单层中掺杂产生显著粒子-空穴不对称性，掺杂与单轴应变结合可在近半填充处产生完全自旋极化的费米面。双层中有利于层间交错磁参量铁性对齐的堆叠使动量依赖自旋劈裂相干叠加；弱非对称掺杂诱导胞内电荷序并产生完全自旋极化的低能载流子。在双层四轨道五电子填充下发现连续顺磁-交错磁相变，强关联下进一步演化为准粒子权重被强烈压制的类Mott区。

Conclusion: 关联效应、应变、掺杂与堆叠可作为调控交错磁金属及产生完全自旋极化费米面的互补手段。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine vanishing net magnetization with a momentum-dependent spin-split electronic structure, providing a route to spin-polarized carriers without ferromagnetism. Here, we investigate how electronic correlations, doping, uniaxial strain, and interlayer coupling control altermagnetism within a minimal Hubbard-type model for mono- and bilayer systems. Comparing Hartree--Fock theory with the rotationally invariant slave-boson (RISB) approach, we demonstrate the robustness of altermagnetic order against quasiparticle renormalization and reveal a separation between the onset of magnetic order and the loss of quasiparticle coherence at stronger coupling. In the monolayer, doping produces a pronounced particle--hole asymmetry, while its combination with uniaxial strain generates a fully spin-polarized Fermi surface close to half-filling. In the bilayer, stacking that favors ferroic alignment of the layer altermagnetic order parameters allows their momentum-dependent spin splittings to combine constructively. Weak asymmetric doping additionally induces intra-unit-cell charge order, resulting in fully spin-polarized low-energy carriers. At a bilayer filling of five electrons in four orbitals, we find a continuous paramagnet-to-altermagnet transition followed, at stronger coupling, by an evolution toward a Mott-like regime with strongly suppressed quasiparticle weight. Our results establish correlations, strain, doping, and stacking as complementary means of controlling altermagnetic metals and generating fully spin-polarized Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [168] [Hund-driven local-itinerant duality of Eu-4$f$ electrons in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23645)
*Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合DFT+DMFT研究Eu掺杂无限层镍基超导112结构中Eu-4f的价态与磁性，揭示其随Hund耦合JH的三阶段演化及局域-巡游二重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eu替代无限层镍酸盐中发现重入超导与Tc升高，其关键问题在于Eu-4f态的性质及其与Ni-3d电子的相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT），同时在Ni-3d与Eu-4f壳层显式处理库仑排斥。

Result: 发现Eu-4f构型随Hund耦合JH呈现三阶段演化：小JH时为非磁态，大JH时为完全自旋极化二价态，仅中等JH下出现实验观测的磁性混合价态，并共存局域矩与强关联j=7/2准粒子。该局域-巡游二重性源于4f内j=5/2与7/2流形间电荷转移形成的有效空穴掺杂轨道选择Mott态。

Conclusion: 该工作解释了Eu离子混合价与局域矩行为的起源，并预言可与Ni-3d杂化的低能4f准粒子可能贡献Tc升高，为理解Eu掺杂无限层镍酸盐的异常铁磁与超导性质提供了基础。

Abstract: Recent discovery of reentrant superconductivity and elevated $T_c$ in Eu-substituted infinite-layer nickelates raises a critical question concerning the nature of Eu-4$f$ states and their potential interaction with Ni-$d$ electrons. Here we combine density functional theory with the dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT) calculations to investigate the valence and magnetic properties of Eu-ions in the nickelate 112 structure, explicitly treating the Coulomb repulsion in both Ni-3$d$ and Eu-4$f$ shells. We find a three-regime evolution of the Eu-4$f$ configuration driven by its Hund's rule coupling $J_{\rm H}$. The magnetic and mixed-valence Eu ionic state inferred by experiments only occurs for moderate $J_{\rm H}$, with coexisting local moments and strongly correlated $j=7/2$ quasiparticles, in contrast to the nonmagnetic state at small $J_{\rm H}$ and the fully spin-polarized divalent state at large $J_{\rm H}$. This local-itinerant duality arises from an effectively hole-doped orbital-selective Mott state induced by the intra-4$f$ charge transfer between $j=5/2$ and $7/2$ manifolds. It not only explains the origin of the mixed-valence and local moment behaviors of the Eu-ions observed experimentally, but also predicts low-energy 4$f$ quasiparticles that may hybridize with the Ni-3$d$ electrons and contribute to the elevated $T_c$. Our work provides a basis for understanding the unusual ferromagnetic and superconducting properties of Eu-substituted infinite-layer nickelates.

</details>


### [169] [Electronic structure and anisotropic magnetotransport in the topological kagome ferromagnet MgMn6Sn6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23699)
*Debasmita Swain,Mohit Mudgal Suman Nandi,Aradhana Kumari,Yogendra Kumar,Vivek Kumar Malik,Anup Pradhan Sakhya,Masahiro Sawada,Shin-ichiro Ideta,Kenya Shimada,Jayita Nayak*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究综合磁性、磁输运与电子结构测量及第一性原理计算，系统表征了kagome铁磁体MgMn6Sn6，建立了其磁各向异性、各向异性磁输运与kagome衍生电子结构之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome材料因Dirac能带交叉、van Hove奇点及拓扑/关联效应而备受关注，但铁磁kagome体系中磁各向异性、磁输运与kagome电子结构之间的关联尚不明确，需要实验与理论结合的系统研究。

Method: 采用磁化测量、角度依赖磁电阻、X射线磁圆二色（XMCD）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）以及第一性原理计算等多种手段对MgMn6Sn6进行表征。

Result: MgMn6Sn6在TC≈295 K附近铁磁有序并具有显著易面磁各向异性；低温低场下磁电阻随场取向强烈各向异性，由面内场的负MR演变为面外场的近二次方行为，并呈现明显二重各向异性及高阶贡献；XMCD显示有限的轨道-自旋矩比，表明SOC作用显著；理论计算给出K点Dirac型能带交叉与M点van Hove奇点；ARPES观测到六重对称费米面及其随结合能的演化，与计算总体一致。

Conclusion: 研究确立了MgMn6Sn6中磁各向异性、各向异性磁输运与kagome衍生电子结构之间的联系，为铁磁kagome体系的自旋-轨道与输运性质研究提供了实验与理论依据。

Abstract: We report the magnetic, magnetotransport, and electronic properties of the kagome ferromag net MgMn6Sn6 using magnetization, angle dependent magnetoresistance, x-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD), angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), and first-principles cal culations. MgMn6Sn6 exhibits ferromagnetic ordering near TC=295 K with pronounced easy plane magnetic anisotropy. At low temperatures and low magnetic fields, the magnetoresistance (MR) is strongly anisotropic with respect to the magnetic field orientation, evolving from a predominantly negative MR for in-plane fields to a more quadratic behavior for out-of-plane fields. Angle depen dent measurements further reveal a pronounced twofold MR anisotropy with additional higher order contributions. The finite orbital-to-spin moment ratio revealed by XMCD suggests a significant role of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in MgMn6Sn6. The first-principles calculations show a Dirac-like band crossing at the K point and a van Hove singularity (VHS) at the M point, as expected for kagome materials. ARPES measurements resolve a sixfold symmetric Fermi surface and its systematic evo lution with binding energy, in overall agreement with first-principles calculations. The measured band dispersions are also broadly consistent with the calculated multiband electronic structure. These results establish the connection between magnetic anisotropy, anisotropic magnetotransport, and the kagome derived electronic structure of MgMn6Sn6.

</details>


### [170] [Decoupling the Magnetic Field Operator as an Independent Operator Class in Stochastic Series Expansion Quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23978)
*Shijie Jin,Lu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出将磁场算符作为独立算符类别的广义SSE量子蒙特卡洛框架，使其平等参与更新并可与其他算符相互转化，在二维反铁磁Heisenberg模型中验证了正确性与竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SSE中磁场项通常被吸收进Heisenberg相互作用，虽在广泛场强下高效，但限制了磁场算符的独立性。作者希望构建更通用的框架，使磁场算符能作为独立算符类别参与蒙特卡洛更新，从而提升测量与计算灵活性。

Method: 在随机级数展开（SSE）量子蒙特卡洛方法中，将磁场算符视为独立算符类别，与其它相互作用算符平等参与loop更新，并允许场算符与相互作用算符在更新过程中相互转化。以二维反铁磁Heisenberg模型在外磁场下为例实现该算法。

Result: 模拟结果与传统算法一致，验证了新表述的正确性；积分自相关时间对比表明新算法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该广义SSE框架在保持效率的同时提供了更大灵活性，既便于测量与算符相关的可观测量，也可能为更广泛问题带来计算优势。

Abstract: The Stochastic Series Expansion (SSE) quantum Monte Carlo method with loop updates is among the most powerful approaches for quantum spin and boson systems. In the standard formulation, the magnetic field term is routinely absorbed into the Heisenberg interactions---a strategy that has proven highly efficient across a wide range of field strengths. In this work, we propose a more general SSE framework in which the magnetic field operator is treated as an independent operator class, enabling it to participate in Monte Carlo updates on an equal footing with all other operators. Importantly, the field operator and other interaction operators can transform into one another during the update process. We demonstrate this algorithm using the two-dimensional antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model in a magnetic field as a concrete example. The simulation results agree with those of the conventional algorithm, confirming the correctness of the new formulation. A comparison of the integrated autocorrelation time shows that the new algorithm is competitive. This flexibility not only facilitates measurements of observables associated with operators but also offers potential computational advantages for a broader class of problems.

</details>


### [171] [A Majorana Formulation of Time-Dependent Two-Particle Reduced-Density-Matrix Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23993)
*Haoran Wang,Alessandro Principi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于相互作用马约拉纳费米子体系的时间依赖两粒子约化密度矩阵(TP-RDM)算法，通过约化三粒子密度矩阵闭合BBGKY层级，并施加保守恒量的正定性投影来稳定演化；在Hubbard模型淬火和Kitaev蜂窝模型磁通动力学上进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时相互作用费米子体系（尤其马约拉纳费米子）的精确动力学模拟在计算上极具挑战，单粒子(如Hartree–Fock)方法无法捕捉强关联效应，因此需要一种介于平均场与全多体方法之间的高效近似框架。

Method: 将BBGKY层级在两粒子层面截断，由低阶关联重构三粒子约化密度矩阵以实现闭合；对两粒子密度矩阵施加正定性投影，同时保持能量等选定守恒量以稳定含时演化；随后在一维Hubbard模型淬火和Kitaev蜂窝模型磁通动力学上进行基准验证。

Result: 在Hubbard淬火中，TP方法捕捉到了Hartree–Fock遗漏的强耦合动力学；在Kitaev淬火中，TP在多个参数区间准确重现早期磁通动力学，当两粒子关联重要时显著优于HF；并进一步表明规范相干性对磁通动力学至关重要。但投影过程引入了有效不可逆性，影响长时动力学。

Conclusion: 两粒子约化密度矩阵方法在捕捉强关联费米子含时动力学方面优于平均场方法，但正定性投影带来的有效不可逆性限制了其在长时行为上的准确性，是未来需要解决的问题。

Abstract: We introduce a time-dependent two-particle (TP) reduced-density-matrix algorithm for systems of interacting Majorana fermions. We close the BBGKY hierarchy at the two-particle level by reconstructing the three-particle reduced density matrix from lower-order correlations. To stabilize the evolution, we impose a positivity projection on the two-particle density matrix while preserving chosen conserved quantities such as the energy. We benchmark the approach on quenches in the one-dimensional Hubbard model and on flux dynamics in the Kitaev honeycomb model. For the Hubbard quench, TP captures strong-coupling dynamics missed by Hartree--Fock (HF). For the Kitaev quenches, TP accurately reproduces the early-time flux dynamics in several regimes and provides a substantial improvement over HF when two-particle correlations are important. We further show that gauge coherence is essential for flux dynamics. The projection procedure however introduces an effective irreversibility, which affects the long-time dynamics.

</details>


### [172] [Carrier duality from the convergence of Dirac fermions and high-order van Hove singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24044)
*Meng Lyu,Kaiyi Zhai,Nikolai Peshcherenko,Junyan Liu,Jinying Yang,Subir Sen,Binbin Wang,Langsheng Ling,Zhaosheng Wang,Gang Li,Jieyi Liu,Yang Xu,Xiyang Li,Claudia Felser,Yang Zhang,Wujun Shi,Lexian Yang,Enke Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在kagome金属Co3In2S2中发现由高迁移率狄拉克费米子与平带重电子共存产生的双载流子区域，为拓扑与关联效应交叉的多体物理提供模型平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高迁移率狄拉克费米子与平带重电子的融合是涌现量子现象的范式，但此类状态的实验实现长期难以达成，亟需找到可实现该状态的候选材料。

Method: 通过磁扭矩量子振荡与角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对kagome金属Co3In2S2的电子结构进行表征，并结合输运与热力学测量，用最小双口袋模型描述狄拉克费米子与高阶范霍夫奇异点（HOVHS）载流子间的相互作用。

Result: 电荷输运由高迁移率电子主导，而热力学响应表现重电子行为；费米面上同时存在拓扑狄拉克费米子与平带四阶Van Hove奇异点（HOVHSs）；相互作用表现为约100 K以上的亚线性电阻率以及低温下的场诱导非费米液体行为。

Conclusion: Co3In2S2可作为探索拓扑与关联效应交叉多体物理的模型平台，并为设计承载多样涌现态的量子材料提供基础框架。

Abstract: The convergence of highly mobile Dirac fermions and flat-band heavy electrons offers a paradigm for emergent quantum phenomena. However, experimental realization of such intriguing state remains elusive. In this study, we report a dual carrier regime in the kagome metal Co3In2S2, wherein the charge transport is governed by high-mobility electrons while the thermodynamic responses exhibit heavy-electron behavior. This exotic duality arises from the coexistence of topological Dirac fermions and flat-band high-order (4th-order) Van Hove singularities (HOVHSs) at the Fermi surface, as revealed by magnetic-torque quantum oscillation and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. The interaction between Dirac and HOVHS-derived carriers can be captured by a minimal two-pocket model, manifesting as sublinear resistivity above ~100 K and field-induced non-Fermi-liquid behavior at low temperatures. Our study establishes Co3In2S2 as a model platform for exploring many-body physics at the intersection of topology and correlation effects, and provides a foundational framework for designing quantum materials hosting diverse emergent states.

</details>


### [173] [Slave-Rotor and Auxiliary Chain Analysis of the Mott Transition in a Modified Periodic Anderson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24315)
*Ankur Majumder,Sudeshna Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用slave-rotor平均场理论研究三轨道修正周期Anderson模型中的Mott-Hubbard转变，引入双格簇刻画短程空间关联，并将动力学自能映射为非相互作用半无限辅助链的边界格林函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mott-Hubbard转变是强电子关联驱动的典型现象，本文旨在探究该转变在此模型中的行为，并揭示短程空间关联对转变点的影响，同时探索该转变可能具有的拓扑性质。

Method: 使用slave-rotor平均场理论；引入最小双格簇扩展以考虑短程空间关联；在slave-rotor分析之外计算动力学自能，并将其映射为非相互作用半无限辅助链的边界格林函数。

Result: 相比单格解，短程空间关联导致转变点发生移动；金属相中，所得跳跃模式对应拓扑平凡的广义Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链。

Conclusion: 该构造为探索该模型中Mott转变可能的拓扑特征提供了框架。

Abstract: The Mott-Hubbard transition is a paradigmatic phenomenon driven by strong electronic correlations. We investigate this transition in a three-orbital modified periodic Anderson model using slave-rotor mean-field theory. A minimal two-site cluster extension is introduced to understand the minimal effects of short-range spatial correlations. In comparison to the single-site solution, short-range spatial correlations reveal a shift of the transition point. Going beyond the slave-rotor analysis, we also evaluate the dynamical self-energy and map it onto the boundary Green's function of a non-interacting semi-infinite auxiliary chain. For the metallic phase, the resulting hopping pattern corresponds to a topologically trivial generalized Su--Schrieffer--Heeger chain. This construction provides a framework for exploring the possible topological character of the Mott transition in this model.

</details>


### [174] [Spin-Orbit Induced Confinement of Correlated Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24429)
*Kai Chen,Junyan Guan,Zhongming Gu,Jie Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在1D Fermi-Hubbard模型中引入自旋轨道耦合可将双占据子（doublon）驱入双粒子散射连续谱；多数杂化衰减，少数保持解耦束缚，形成相互作用诱导的连续谱束缚态（BICs），并通过耦合声学腔阵列实验观测到辐射连续谱与局域BIC态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排斥束缚的双占据子通常孤立于散射连续谱之外；自旋轨道耦合会改变能带结构，提供将束缚对移向连续谱的调控手段，但进入该区域一般导致即刻解离，能否在保持束缚性质的同时将其驱入连续谱是未解决的基础难题。

Method: 基于一维Fermi-Hubbard模型引入自旋轨道耦合，求解相互作用两粒子问题；将两粒子问题映射为二维耦合声学腔晶格，并在实验上观测双粒子连续谱与束缚态。

Result: 自旋轨道耦合可将双占据子驱入双粒子散射连续谱；多数态与扩展通道杂化并衰减，而部分态保持解耦且空间束缚，形成多体连续谱束缚态（BICs）；实验观测到辐射双占据子连续谱与受限BIC态。

Conclusion: 自旋轨道耦合可将特定双占据子转化为相互作用诱导的BICs，深化了对相互作用束缚对连续谱物理的理解。

Abstract: Repulsively bound doublons are two-particle composites formed by strong interactions and are usually separated from the scattering continuum. Introducing spin-orbit coupling fundamentally alters the underlying band structure, providing a powerful tuning knob to shift these isolated pairs toward this continuum. However, because entering such a regime typically dictates immediate dissociation, whether this coupling can drive these pairs inside while preserving their bound nature constitutes a fundamental unresolved challenge. Here we show that spin-orbit coupling in the one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model can drive doublons into the two-particle scattering continuum. Most of these states hybridize with extended channels and decay, whereas a subset remains decoupled and spatially bound, forming many-body bound states in the continuum (BICs). We map the interacting two-particle problem onto a two-dimensional lattice of coupled acoustic cavities, and experimentally observe both the radiating doublon continuum and the confined BIC states. These results demonstrate that spin-orbit coupling can turn selected doublons into interaction-induced BICs, deepening the understanding of continuum physics for interaction-bound pairs.

</details>


### [175] [Charge-4e Superconducting Ground State without Pair Condensation: Exact Quartet Dynamics, Rigorous Order, and a Microscopic Route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24445)
*Jin-Tao Jin,Pengfei Li,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构建并精确求解了直接电荷4e超导的最小母体模型，证明存在四电子相干序而无电荷2e配对凝聚，并通过强耦合机制用电子跃迁和两体相互作用实现了该物态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接电荷4e超导体展现四电子相干序而所有电荷2e配对通道均不凝聚，但其微观母体模型、严格判据及可由基本电子自由度实现的机制尚不明确，本文旨在建立互补的三项结果来填补这一空白。

Method: 一，基于η团簇态与二分配母体，在任意连通图上构建并精确求解最小两项母体，通过映射到经典硬核排斥动力学获得固定扇区能隙和四重态密度模支。二，对具有显式四重态转移和足够大在位惩罚的有限程费米子族，在半四重态填充下严格证明四重态ODLRO无电荷2e ODLRO。三，仅用电子跃迁与两体相互作用推导强耦合实现，分析局域能隙U0、对跃迁K与电子跃迁t的竞争阶数。

Result: 母体的固定粒子数基态具有四重态ODLRO而无电荷2e ODLRO；非零四重态刚度和消失的四重态逆压缩率将该z=2母体识别为相分离边界。费米子族在超立方XY点(d≥2)及方格子有限XXZ区间均严格成立。强耦合下K以K^2/U0阶产生四重态运动，t首次在t^4/U0^3阶贡献，电荷2e激发在O(U0)保持能隙；二分配格子上正逆压缩率开启渐近可控的z=1均匀区，负曲率驱动相分离。

Conclusion: 本文建立了直接电荷4e超导的三项互补理论结果，从可积母体、严格费米子证明到强耦合实现，系统刻画了无电荷2e凝聚条件下四重态ODLRO的出现及其相图，为四电子超导提供了微观基础。

Abstract: A direct charge-\(4e\) superconductor exhibits coherent four-electron order while every charge-\(2e\) pairing channel remains uncondensed. We establish three complementary results. First, building on the \(η\)-clustering states and bipartite parent of Yoshida and Katsura, we formulate and exactly solve a minimal two-term parent on any connected graph. Its fixed-number ground states have quartet off-diagonal long-range order (ODLRO) without charge-\(2e\) ODLRO, while an exact mapping to classical hard-core exclusion dynamics yields the full fixed-sector gap and a branch of quartet-density modes. Nonzero quartet stiffness and vanishing inverse quartet compressibility identify this \(z=2\) parent as a phase-separation boundary. Second, for a finite-range fermionic family with explicit quartet transfer and sufficiently large onsite penalty, we rigorously prove quartet ODLRO without charge-\(2e\) ODLRO at half quartet filling, both at the hypercubic XY point for \(d\geq2\) and throughout a finite XXZ interval on the square lattice. Third, we derive a strong-coupling realization using only electron hopping and two-body interactions. With local gap \(U_0\), pair hopping \(K\) generates quartet motion at order \(K^2/U_0\), whereas electron hopping \(t\) first contributes at order \(t^4/U_0^3\); charge-\(2e\) excitations remain gapped at \(O(U_0)\). On bipartite lattices, positive inverse quartet compressibility opens an asymptotically controlled homogeneous \(z=1\) regime with short-ranged pair correlations, while negative curvature drives phase separation.

</details>


### [176] [Carrier-mediated spin helices in low-dimensional systems: Josephson-interference features and correlation renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24502)
*Yung-Yeh Chang,Chen-Hsuan Hsu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合线滑动Luttinger液体中的载流子诱导螺旋磁性，通过变分处理与Josephson结输运探测揭示螺旋交换场对载流子重构及干涉图样的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 载流子诱导的螺旋磁性可在与局域磁矩耦合的一维相互作用体系中产生，但其在耦合线滑动Luttinger液体中的对应形式（含/不含谷自由度）尚不明确，且缺乏对螺旋纹理的输运探测手段。

Method: 以孤立Tomonaga-Luttinger液体为参考极限，研究含与不含谷自由度的耦合线滑动Luttinger液体；利用载流子自旋磁化率中介的RKKY相互作用选择螺旋序波矢，通道间关联锁定相对螺旋相位；采用变分处理估计螺旋诱导的部分能隙并分析剩余无能隙载流子模式的重构；将螺旋嵌入各向异性超导体-正常金属-超导体Josephson结（受twisted bilayer WTe2耦合通道结构启发），利用结界面附近的磁通聚焦使面内场成为自旋螺旋的可控探针。

Result: 螺旋交换场改变了中心瓣宽度与相对瓣权重；在横向通道几何中产生场依赖的侧瓣不对称与中心峰位移，而在匹配参考体系中这些效应可忽略；重构的载流子部分表现出修正的局域态密度指数、自旋弛豫行为和竞争关联，在孤立与耦合线体系中呈现不同特征。

Conclusion: 该工作将耦合线体系中的集体螺旋序与载流子部分重构联系起来，并确立场可调Josephson干涉测量作为空间自旋纹理的互补输运探针。

Abstract: Carrier-induced helical magnetism can arise in interacting one-dimensional systems coupled to localized moments. Here, we investigate the coupled-wire sliding-Luttinger-liquid counterparts with and without valley degrees of freedom, using isolated Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids as reference limits. The carrier spin susceptibility mediates a Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida interaction that selects the helical ordering wave vector, while interchannel correlations lock the relative helix phases and stabilize a collective quasi-two-dimensional texture. Using a variational treatment, we estimate the helix-induced partial gap and analyze the reconstruction of the remaining gapless carrier modes. To probe the spatial texture through transport, we incorporate the helix into an anisotropic superconductor-normal-metal-superconductor Josephson junction motivated by the coupled-channel structure of twisted bilayer WTe$_2$. We exploit flux focusing near the junction interfaces to make an applied in-plane field a controlled probe of the spin helix. Relative to junctions without the helix, the helical exchange field modifies the central-lobe width and relative lobe weights. In the transverse-channel geometry, it also produces field-dependent side-lobe asymmetry and central-peak displacement, which remain negligible in the matched references for the parameters considered. The reconstructed carrier sector also exhibits modified local-density-of-states exponents, spin-relaxation behavior, and competing correlations, with distinct signatures in isolated and coupled-wire systems. Our results connect collective helical order in coupled-wire systems to carrier-sector reconstruction and identify field-tunable Josephson interferometry as a complementary transport probe of the spatial spin texture.

</details>


### [177] [Raman Scattering Evidence for Fluctuating Kagome-Plane Moments in $\mathrm{B} \parallel [111]$ in Pr$_2$Zr$_2$O$_7$ pyrochlore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24696)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Huiyuan Man,Nan Tang,Li Xiang,Sami Muhammad,Komalavalli Thirunavukkuarasu,Dmitry Smirnov,Satoru Nakatsuji,Natalia Drichko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过磁场下的磁拉曼光谱研究Pr₂Zr₂O₇中Pr³⁺的晶体电场激发，发现[111]方向下kagome晶格上的CEF激发不分裂而展宽，符合毫电子伏特时间尺度的磁矩涨落（运动窄化），解释了磁化强度降低并支持偶极-四极矩图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烧绿石Pr₂Zr₂O₇具有由自旋、轨道和晶格自由度耦合产生的奇异磁性，其磁矩的偶极-四极矩性质与非Kramers双重态的劈裂均被用于讨论其奇异行为，但缺乏对晶体电场激发及其磁场演化的直接谱学探测，因此需要实验区分这些机制。

Method: 在2 K下、最高14 T磁场沿[100]和[111]晶向，利用磁拉曼光谱探测Pr³⁺的晶体电场(CEF)激发，并分析拉曼活性CEF模的磁场演化，包括高温下热布居的上Zeeman支。

Result: B∥[100]时CEF模的场演化可由Pr³⁺基态双重态的传统Zeeman劈裂定量描述；B∥[111]时谱线分离为三角与kagome子晶格的响应：三角晶格上Pr³⁺的CEF激发呈现与完全磁矩极化一致的正确Zeeman位移，而kagome晶格上的CEF激发不分裂，反而随磁场增强而显著展宽，与静态极化构型不符，可用kagome面内磁矩在Zeeman劈裂能级间以meV时间尺度涨落的运动窄化模型唯象描述。

Conclusion: 该结果为B∥[111]下观测到的磁化强度降低提供了自然解释，并进一步支持磁矩的偶极-四极矩性质，表明拉曼散射是探测阻挫磁体中奇异自旋-轨道动力学的灵敏手段。

Abstract: The exotic magnetic properties of the pyrochlore Pr$_2$Zr$_2$O$_7$ are determined by the coupling of spin, orbital, and lattice degrees of freedom. Magnetism in this material originates from the non-Kramers Pr$^{3+}$ ion, and the exotic behavior has been discussed both in the framework of dipole-quadrupole properties of magnetic moments and disorder splitting the non-Kramers doublet. We use magneto-Raman spectroscopy to probe the crystal electric field (CEF) excitations of Pr$^{3+}$ in magnetic fields up to 14 T applied along the [100] and [111] crystallographic directions at 2 K. For $\mathrm{B}\parallel[100]$, the field evolution of the Raman active crystal field modes is quantitatively described by conventional Zeeman splitting of the Pr$^{3+}$ ground state doublet, including the thermally populated upper Zeeman branch at elevated temperature. For $\mathrm{}{B}\parallel[111]$, the spectra separate into responses from the triangular and kagome sublattices, and the CEF excitations of Pr$^{3+}$ on the triangular lattice exhibit the expected Zeeman shift consistent with full moments polarization. In contrast, the CEF excitations of Pr$^{3+}$ on the kagome lattice do not split as expected and instead broaden strongly with increasing field. This behavior is inconsistent with a static polarized configuration and is captured phenomenologically by a motional-narrowing description in which the kagome plane moments fluctuate between Zeeman-split levels on a meV timescale. This result offers a natural explanation for the reduced magnetization observed in $\mathrm{}{B}\parallel[111]$ and provides further evidence of the dipole-quadrupole properties of magnetic moments. It highlights Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of exotic spin-orbital dynamics in frustrated magnets.

</details>


### [178] [Infrared evidence for strong $C_{3}$ symmetry breaking in 1$T$-TiSe$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24705)
*Esther van Grondelle,Kai Rossnagel,Jasper van Wezel,Erik van Heumen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用红外光谱探测1T-TiSe₂电荷密度波相变，发现双重简并Eu声子分裂，直接证明三重旋转对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中晶格与电子自由度的相互作用导致竞争有序态及后续对称性破缺相变，各相差异细微但可通过剩余点群对称性区分；作者希望明确1T-TiSe₂电荷密度波相变过程中三重旋转对称性是否破缺，以检验关于手性/向列相中间态的模型。

Method: 采用红外光学光谱技术，在1T-TiSe₂的电荷密度波相变（T_CDW≈190 K）处探测对称性破缺，观测双重简并Eu光学声子的分裂行为，并结合声子线宽分析。

Result: 观察到T_CDW≈190 K处此前未发现的Eu双重简并光学声子分裂，为三重旋转对称性破缺提供直接证据；两个原Eu伴峰的能量差只能由与键长变化同量级的大C₃破缺畸变解释；T_CDW以上单声子模式的线宽可能与三重对称性亦被破缺的涨落态一致。

Conclusion: 该发现排除了CDW向低温手性或向列相通过保持三重对称性中间态转变的模型，表明C₃对称性破缺在CDW相变中起主导作用。

Abstract: The interplay between lattice and electron degrees of freedom gives rise to competing ordered states in quantum materials, which can lead to a series of subsequent symmetry breaking transitions. While the differences are often subtle, these phases can be distinguished by their remaining point group symmetries. Here, we use infrared optical spectroscopy to probe symmetry breaking at the charge density wave transition of 1$T$-TiSe$_{2}$. We uncover a previously unobserved splitting of a doubly degenerate $E_u$ optical phonon at $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}\approx$ 190 K that constitutes direct evidence for the breaking of three-fold rotational symmetry. Our finding rules out proposals where the CDW transition to a low temperature chiral or nematic phase takes place through an intermediary state that preserves three-fold symmetry. The energy difference between the two former $E_{u}$ partners can only be explained by large $C_{3}$ breaking distortions of the same order of magnitude as previously observed changes in bond lengths, suggesting that $C_{3}$ symmetry breaking plays a dominant role in the CDW transition. The linewidth of the single phonon mode above $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$ could be consistent with a fluctuating state where three-fold symmetry is also broken.

</details>


### [179] [Rapidly prototyping kagome flat band physics with acoustic metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24721)
*Jiatong Yang,Benjamin H. November,Yiting Huang,S. Minhal Gardezi,Harris Pirie,Jennifer E. Hoffman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于声学超材料的平带系统快速原型工作流，以空气腔为格点、通道调控跃迁，直接实现紧束缚模型，并在kagome及其扩展晶格中实现平带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带（零群速度、动能淬灭）是关联态与非常规超导等新奇物态的重要平台，但在量子材料中设计并表征平带耗时且昂贵，极大限制了平带系统设计空间的探索。声学超材料可仿真、易制备、测量快，是有吸引力的替代方案。

Method: 构建完整工作流：用空气腔作为格点、用连接通道控制跃迁，从而直接实现紧束缚模型，并可推广到多种晶格几何。以kagome晶格为概念验证，比较紧束缚理论、有限元仿真与实验测量；并设计一系列扩展kagome晶格，通过新增格点逐级抵消更长程跃迁以压平平带。

Result: 在kagome晶格中，紧束缚理论、有限元仿真与实验结果高度一致；扩展kagome家族中，新增格点可逐级抵消更长程跃迁并使平带更平坦。制备与测量仅需数小时而非数月，总成本比量子材料低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该声学超材料平台可实现平带系统的快速迭代与筛选，加速发现量子材料中的新平带物理。

Abstract: Flat bands with vanishing group velocity and quenched kinetic energy provide fertile ground for correlated states and exotic phenomena such as unconventional superconductivity. Yet, engineering and characterizing flat bands in quantum materials is time-consuming and costly, limiting the exploration of the vast design space of possible flat band systems. Acoustic metamaterials offer an accessible alternative: they can be easily simulated, cheaply fabricated, and quickly measured. Here we present a complete workflow to rapidly prototype flat band systems using acoustic metamaterials. Our design directly implements a tight-binding model using air cavities as lattice sites connected by channels that control hopping, allowing it to generalize to diverse lattice geometries. Using the kagome lattice as a proof-of-concept, we demonstrate excellent agreement between tight-binding theory, finite-element simulations, and experimental measurements. We then design a family of extended kagome lattices whose added sites cancel successively longer-range hopping, flattening the flat band. With fabrication and measurement requiring hours rather than months and a total cost orders of magnitude lower than quantum materials, our approach enables rapid iteration through candidate lattices, facilitating the discovery of new flat band physics in quantum materials.

</details>


### [180] [Anisotropic Surface State Band Splitting and Low Energy Flat Bands in 3d Correlated Topological Kondo Insulator Candidate FeSb$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24854)
*Ziling Cao,Jie Pang,Yu Xu,Taimin Miao,Bo Liang,Wenpei Zhu,Neng Cai,Mingkai Xu,Jumin Shi,Yingjie Shu,Yiwen Chen,Jiachen Wang,Shenjin Zhang,Fengfeng Zhang,Feng Yang,Zhimin Wang,Qinjun Peng,Zhihai Zhu,Xintong Li,Hanqing Mao,Guodong Liu,Zuyan Xu,Youguo Shi,Lin Zhao,X. J. Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用高分辨激光ARPES研究了关联窄隙半导体FeSb$_2$的电子结构，发现区心表面态呈各向异性劈裂，降温至电阻平台区（<6 K）时表面态峰变锐并增重，且出现两个平带-like特征（~127 meV仅沿特定高对称方向，~70 meV沿所有动量切面），为理解其重整化效应、电阻平台及拓扑性质提供新信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeSb$_2$是被广泛讨论的3d电子Kondo绝缘体候选材料，表现出低温电阻平台及可能的表面主导导电行为，但其电子结构细节尚不清晰，亟需高分辨实验加以澄清。

Method: 采用系统的高分辨激光基角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对FeSb$_2$的电子结构进行测量，并在降温进入电阻平台区（<6 K）时对比表面态谱型与谱重变化。

Result: 区心附近表面态显示清晰各向异性劈裂；降温进入电阻平台区后表面态依然稳健，但光发射峰显著变锐并增加谱重；在低能处观察到两个不同的平带-like特征：一个约位于费米能级下127 meV且仅沿特定高对称方向出现，另一个约位于费米能级下70 meV且在区心附近所有测量动量切面上均存在。

Conclusion: 这些结果加深了对FeSb$_2$重整化效应、电阻平台机理及拓扑性质的理解，表明其低能电子结构与表面态密切相关。

Abstract: FeSb$_2$ is a correlated narrow-gap semiconductor that has often been discussed as a $3d$-electron Kondo insulator candidate and exhibits a low-temperature resistance plateau with possible surface-dominated conduction. We carried out a systematic high-resolution laser-based angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) study of FeSb$_2$ to investigate its electronic structure. The surface states around the zone center show clear anisotropic splitting. When the temperature is lowered into the resistance plateau regime ($<6\,\mathrm{K}$), the surface states remain robust, but their photoemission peaks become much sharper and gain spectral weight. Two distinct flat-band-like features are observed at low energy. One is located at $\sim$127 meV below the Fermi level, which exists only along a specific high-symmetry direction, while the other is located at $\sim$70 meV below the Fermi level and is present along all the measured momentum cuts around the zone center. These results provide new information to understand the renormalization effects, the resistance plateau, and the topological nature of FeSb$_2$.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum-interference-driven orbital density wave and high-temperature superconductivity in trilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24857)
*Youichi Yamakawa,Hiroshi Kontani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出密度波方程（含顶点修正），揭示三层镍酸盐中短程SDW涨落的量子干涉诱导外层间键序，进而产生内层轨道序，统一解释了交织密度波序与高温超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温超导多层镍酸盐中CDW与SDW交织共存，且Qcdw≈2Qsdw，其微观起源及与超导的关系尚不明确，需要统一的理论框架。

Method: 采用含顶点修正的密度波方程，研究外层NiO2面上q≈Qsdw短程SDW涨落之间的量子干涉效应。

Result: 短程SDW涨落干涉在外层间产生Qcdw≈2Qsdw的键序，诱导以内层为中心的轨道序，Ni d_{3z^2-r^2}与d_{x^2-y^2}占据呈反相调制，轨道极化强而总电荷调制弱；结果与NMR/NQR、拉曼光谱和STM一致。

Conclusion: 键-轨道交织序解释层选择性电子重构，且轨道与自旋涨落通过Mz镜像宇称选择规则协同稳定s±波超导，为多层镍酸盐中交织密度波序与高温超导提供统一微观框架。

Abstract: Intertwined charge-density-wave (CDW) and spin-density-wave (SDW) orders are a hallmark of high-temperature superconducting multilayer nickelates. In trilayer La4Ni3O_{10}, charge correlations develop at temperatures above the onset of long-range spin order, and the characteristic ordering wavevectors satisfy $Q_{cdw} \approx 2Q_{sdw}$. Here, using a density-wave equation with vertex corrections, we show that quantum interference between short-range SDW fluctuations at $q \approx Q_{sdw}$ on the outer NiO2 layers generates an inter-outer-layer bond order at $Q_{cdw} \approx 2 Q_{sdw}$. This bond order induces a pronounced inner-layer-centered orbital order, with antiphase modulations of the Ni $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ occupations, producing strong orbital polarization but only weak total charge modulation. This intertwined bond-and-orbital order accounts for the layer-selective electronic reconstruction inferred from NMR/NQR and is consistent with Raman spectroscopy and scanning tunnelling microscopy measurements. The same orbital and spin fluctuations also cooperate to stabilize $s_{\pm}$-wave superconductivity through $M_z$ mirror-parity selection rules. Our results provide a unified microscopic framework for intertwined density-wave order and high-Tc superconductivity in multilayer nickelates.

</details>


### [182] [Staged emergence of anomalous Hall transport in a correlated uranium Weyl semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24897)
*Sabin Regmi,Shuxiang Zhou,Chandan K. Singh,Alexei Fedorov,Jonathan Denlinger,Zeyu Ma,Yidi Wang,Jennifer E. Hoffman,Peter M. Oppeneer,Dariusz Kaczorowski,Tomasz Durakiewicz,Krzysztof Gofryk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过磁输运、角分辨光电子能谱、热力学测量与第一性原理计算，研究铀铁磁体UPS中电子关联对拓扑态的改造，揭示了磁有序、关联电子重构与异常霍尔输运发生在不同但重叠的温区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子关联如何重塑拓扑量子态是量子材料领域的核心难题，而铀5f电子兼具巡游与局域的双重特性，是研究该问题的理想平台。

Method: 结合磁输运测量、角分辨光电子能谱（含共振光电子谱与变温测量）、热力学测量以及第一性原理计算，对铀铁磁体UPS进行多手段联合表征。

Result: 共振光电子谱在费米能级处观测到窄的U-5f谱权重与宽的非相干态共存；异常霍尔电导率约为4.5×10² Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，且并不简单跟随有序磁矩；变温光电子谱显示T_C=118 K附近变化较小，而在约90 K以下出现明显的低能5f谱权重重分布；第一性原理计算识别出具有显著贝里曲率的对称性保护Weyl交叉点，并给出约9.6×10² Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹的本征异常霍尔电导率。

Conclusion: 磁有序、关联电子重构与异常霍尔输运在铀Weyl半金属中于不同但相互重叠的温区发展，表明强关联能够重塑拓扑输运行为。

Abstract: Understanding how electronic correlations reshape topological states remains a central challenge in quantum materials. Here we investigate the uranium ferromagnet UPS using magnetotransport, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, thermodynamic measurements, and first principles calculations. Resonant photoemission reveals narrow U-$5f$ spectral weight at the Fermi level coexisting with broad incoherent states, consistent with the itinerant/localized duality characteristic of uranium $5f$ electrons. The anomalous Hall conductivity reaches approximately $4.5\times10^{2}\,Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$, yet does not simply follow the ordered magnetic moment. Temperature dependent photoemission reveals relatively little change across $T_C=118$~K, followed by a pronounced redistribution of low-energy $5f$ spectral weight below approximately 90~K. First-principles calculations identify a symmetry protected Weyl crossing with pronounced Berry curvature and yield an intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity of approximately $9.6\times10^{2}\,Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate that magnetic order, correlated electronic reconstruction, and anomalous Hall transport develop over distinct but overlapping temperature ranges, revealing how strong correlations reshape topological transport in a uranium Weyl semimetal.

</details>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Testing Two-State Access in Brain and Language Model: Human EEG Reproduction, Simulation Audit, and a Proposed Model Assay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22300)
*Pieter van Rooyen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文检讨"人工系统能否支持意识"的可行路径:以全局神经元工作空间理论(GNWT)关于阈限附近单试次反应分布应为"两态混合"而非连续体的预测为切入点,复现已发表的竞争模型检验,并为把该检验迁移到语言模型表征而校准一个留出族比较方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑与人工神经网络硬件同为普通物质,其组织方式是否足以支持意识尚无定论。作者不直接断言意识,而是选取GNWT的一个具体可检验预测,试图给出一条可复现、可迁移的判定路径。

Method: (1) 依据原作者代码,在二十名被试的公开EEG数据上复现"两态模型 vs 连续体模型"的竞争模型检验,并按被试的主动/被动会话分别分析,采用protected exceedance probability(PXP)与逐窗比较,辅以梯度型比较模型作对照。(2) 为迁移至语言模型表征,设计留出族比较(held-out family comparison),在单层仿真中用十二种梯度型零假设、共12,000个数据集进行校准,包括概念簇区间的覆盖率检验。另提出含自然文本证据剂量、目标桥接与状态条件因果检验的模型研究方案,但未实施。

Result: 主动会话中,两态模型的PXP在同样的315 ms窗口首次超过0.95,预测优势约0.003 nat/trial,且区间边界对优化器敏感;被动会话中,额外的梯度假说比较模型排名最高,但未做出跨窗判定。留出族比较在仿真中于12,000个数据集、十二种梯度型零假设下未出现假的两态判定,但其概念簇区间在六种设置下覆盖不足,最低降至0.22,故该区间在确证性使用前必须替换并重新验证。

Conclusion: 复现结果与原文在关键窗口和方向上一致,但证据强度有限且对分析选择敏感;面向语言模型的迁移工具尚未达标。作者未对意识体验作任何主张,仅将全局工作空间的可及性作为一种候选机制加以方法学检验。

Abstract: Brains and the hardware of artificial neural networks are both organized ordinary matter, and whether an artificial system's organization can support consciousness remains open. This paper examines one proposed mechanism, access to a capacity-limited global workspace, through one prediction of the global neuronal workspace theory: near threshold, the single-trial response distribution is a mixture of two states rather than a continuum. Two completed analyses are reported. First, the published competing-model test of that prediction is reproduced from the authors' code on the open EEG data of twenty participants. In the active session the two-state model's protected exceedance probability first exceeds 0.95 at the same 315 ms window, with a modest predictive advantage of about 0.003 nat per trial and optimizer-sensitive interval edges; in the passive session, an additional analysis, a graded comparator ranks highest without an across-window decision. Second, a held-out family comparison proposed for transferring the test to language-model representations is calibrated in simulation at one layer: it made no false two-state call in 12,000 datasets under twelve graded nulls, but its concept-cluster interval under-covered in six settings, down to 0.22, so a replacement interval must be validated before any confirmatory use. The proposed model study, with a natural-text evidence dose, a target bridge and a state-conditional causal test, is specified but not run. No claim about experience is made.

</details>


### [184] [Towards the spin-glass transition in finite dimensions via blue percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22301)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文在高有限维零场Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃的Chayes-Machta-Redner表示中证明了蓝色渗流现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究零场Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃在高有限维下的渗流行为，旨在刻画自旋玻璃相变与蓝扇区密度不平衡、自旋翻转相关Gibbs态及压力尖点之间的联系。

Method: 在Chayes-Machta-Redner表示框架下研究蓝色渗流；对固定对称iid耦合（0<m=E|J|<∞）分析周期联合极限中的首次渗流阈值；对对称±1耦合给出计算机辅助的22维验证；对指数矩无序建立渗流区制。

Result: 首次渗流起始渐近于(2dm)^{-1}；对对称±1耦合获得22维计算机辅助证书；对指数矩无序建立了无限蓝扇区密度严格相等且自旋玻璃磁化率有限的渗流区制。在此周期极限中，持续的密度不平衡将意味着存在不同的自旋翻转相关Gibbs态以及适当有限局域复本耦合下的压力尖点，而无需有限蓝抵消。

Conclusion: 证明了高有限维下蓝色渗流及相应阈值与区制性质，但密度不平衡的证明仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We prove blue percolation in the Chayes-Machta-Redner representation of the zero-field Edwards-Anderson spin glass in high finite dimensions. For fixed symmetric iid couplings with $0<m=\mathbb{E}|J|<\infty$, the first percolation onset is asymptotic to $(2dm)^{-1}$ in periodic joint limits. For symmetric $\pm1$ couplings, we give a computer-assisted dimension-22 certificate. For exponential-moment disorder, we also establish a percolating regime with exactly equal infinite-blue-sector densities and finite spin-glass susceptibility. In these periodic limits, persistent density imbalance would imply distinct spin-flip-related Gibbs states and a pressure cusp for a suitable finite local replica coupling, without requiring finite-blue cancellation. Proving imbalance remains open.

</details>


### [185] [A rank-two spherical transform for eigenvector and singular-vector overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22334)
*Pierre Bousseyroux*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文定义球面 H-变换的秩二级扩展 H^(2) 及其伴随变换 R^(2)，用于计算两个 Hermite 化预解式平均乘积的 4×4 公式，并推广至相关矩阵对的特征向量/奇异向量重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典球面 H-变换处理单个矩阵，难以刻画两个预解式乘积及非对角特征向量重叠；需要更高秩扩展以统一并恢复 Ginibre、椭圆、双不变等既有公式。

Method: 引入秩二级扩展 H^(2) 与伴随变换 R^(2)，对和运算保持可加性；由 R^(2) 导出 4×4 预解式平均乘积公式，进而处理相关矩阵对的重叠。

Result: 给出两 Hermite 化预解式平均乘积的 4×4 公式，可算非对角特征向量重叠，恢复 Ginibre、椭圆、双不变公式；并得到相关矩阵对之间特征向量或奇异向量的重叠。

Conclusion: 秩二级扩展统一并推广了球面 H-变换框架，为预解式乘积与非对角重叠提供计算工具，适用于非 Hermitian 及相关矩阵系综。

Abstract: We define a rank-two extension $\mathcal{H}^{(2)}$ of the spherical $\mathcal{H}$-transform, together with its associated transform $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$. For sums, both transforms are additive. The transform $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$ yields a $4\times4$ formula for the average product of two hermitized resolvents, and hence for off-diagonal eigenvector overlaps, recovering the Ginibre, elliptic, and bi-invariant formulas. We also treat correlated pairs of matrices, and obtain overlaps between their eigenvectors or singular vectors.

</details>


### [186] [Gradient-estimator design overcomes trainability barriers in neural-network-based variational optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22342)
*Yi-Ran Xue,Rui Wang,Baigeng Wang,Chenan Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文针对神经网络变分优化中弱梯度区训练失败的问题，提出无偏直接梯度估计器及自适应最小方差相位（AMVP）估计器，通过提升弱梯度信噪比，在量子多体物理与从头量子化学中实现可靠且低成本的精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络虽具备强表达能力，但在弱梯度区训练易失败，限制了其在量子多体物理与从头量子化学等科学计算中的实际应用。因此需要从梯度估计器设计入手，提升弱梯度的信噪比以实现可靠优化。

Method: 推导无偏直接梯度估计器，并提出自适应最小方差相位（AMVP）估计器，用于神经网络变分优化，改善弱梯度的信噪比并降低计算成本。

Result: 在关联通量模型上，紧凑网络以低于标准估计器模型一个数量级以上的GPU时间，超越更大且经微调的默认标准估计器模型，并超过DMRG精度；在N2键断裂中达到化学精度，并首次在含显式自旋轨道耦合的重元素I2中达到化学精度。

Conclusion: 梯度估计器设计可拓展神经网络变分方法的能力，使其适用于需要高精度的科学计算，并显著降低计算代价。

Abstract: Neural networks provide expressive representations for scientific computing. However, even sufficiently expressive networks can suffer training failure in weak-gradient regimes, limiting their practical use in quantum many-body physics and ab initio quantum chemistry. Here we derive an unbiased direct gradient estimator and introduce the adaptive minimum-variance phase (AMVP) estimator for neural-network variational optimization. By improving the signal-to-noise ratio of weak gradients, these methods enable reliable scientific calculations where training previously failed, while substantially reducing computational cost. The framework enables compact networks to outperform larger and fine-tuned default standard-estimator models with over an order of magnitude less GPU time on correlated flux models, and ultimately exceed the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) accuracy. It further achieves chemical accuracy in N$_2$ bond breaking and, for the first time, in heavy-element I$_2$ with explicit spin-orbit coupling. These results demonstrate that gradient-estimator design expands the capabilities of neural-network variational methods for accurate scientific computing.

</details>


### [187] [Extreme-Scale Ising Machines with Cluster Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22645)
*Xiuqi Zhang,Shuvro Chowdhury,Shaila Niazi,Christian Z. Pratt,Navid Anjum Aadit,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类平均场理论（CMFT），将大交互图按硬件容量分块，各簇独立更新，跨簇交互通过边界自旋平均值计算的有效偏置传递，并引入动态分区轮换以降低固定边界误差。在三维自旋玻璃与植入式Pegasus实例上，残余能量随扫描预算呈幂律下降；结合自动图划分器与加权恢复比启发式选择分区；在四块GPU、约四百万p-bit上实现与单GPU全图实现相当的能密且最快约15倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将模拟与数字Ising机扩展到更大问题规模面临器件容量有限和器件间通信开销两大瓶颈，需要一种既能突破单设备容量限制、又能控制通信成本且保持解质量的框架。

Method: CMFT将大交互图划分为可适配现有硬件的簇；各簇独立进行局部更新，跨簇耦合通过由边界自旋平均值周期性更新的有效偏置实现。为缓解固定簇边界带来的近似误差，提出动态分区，循环使用多个划分，使某阶段被平均场近似的交互在另一阶段以瞬时自旋形式参与。并提出自动图划分器与加权恢复比（weighted recovery ratio）启发式，用于在无自然割方向的图上选择分区组合。实验在四块GPU、约四百万p-bit上验证。

Result: 在三维自旋玻璃和植入式Pegasus实例上，动态CMFT的残余能量随扫描预算呈幂律衰减，表明尽管存在平均场近似，解质量仍随计算投入持续改善。四GPU、约四百万p-bit的实现达到与单GPU全图实现相当的能密，且最快约15倍加速。自动图划分结合加权恢复比为缺乏自然割方向的图提供了实用的分区选择启发式。

Conclusion: 通过可编程有效偏置耦合局部演化的簇，CMFT为在模拟与数字硬件上构建超大规模Ising机提供了一条可行路径：以分而治之方式突破容量与通信瓶颈，并在动态分区下实现解质量随算力持续提升。

Abstract: Scaling analog and digital Ising machines to larger problems requires overcoming finite device capacity and the cost of communication between devices. We present cluster mean-field theory (CMFT), a framework that partitions a large interaction graph into clusters sized to fit available hardware. Each cluster performs local updates independently, while interactions across cluster boundaries enter through periodically updated effective biases computed from boundary-spin averages. To reduce the error introduced by fixed cluster boundaries, we introduce dynamic partitioning, which cycles through multiple partitions so that interactions approximated by mean fields at one stage can act through instantaneous spins at another. On three-dimensional spin glasses and planted Pegasus instances, dynamic CMFT exhibits power-law decay of residual energy over sweep budgets. This shows that solution quality continues to improve with computational effort despite the mean-field approximation. An automated graph partitioner combined with a weighted recovery ratio provides a practical heuristic for selecting partition combinations on graphs without natural cut directions. We demonstrate CMFT on four GPUs with approximately four million p-bits, reaching comparable energy densities up to 15 times faster than a single-GPU implementation of the full graph. By coupling locally evolving clusters through programmable effective biases, CMFT provides a route to extreme-scale Ising machines on both analog and digital hardware.

</details>


### [188] [The disordered logistic map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24402)
*Joseph W. Baron,Tobias Galla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究构建了具有无序耦合的多逻辑斯蒂映射系统，发现极小无序即可消除传统周期倍增级联，转而出现高维混沌（伴随一种此前未报道的振荡不稳定性）；耦合足够强且均匀时可部分恢复经典级联行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑斯蒂映射是低维混沌的经典模型，而高维混沌多见于具有大量相互作用、异质耦合组分的无序系统。作者旨在探究无序耦合如何影响经典的周期倍增级联，并揭示低维动力学与高维无序系统之外的新现象。

Method: 构建由多个逻辑斯蒂映射通过无序耦合相互作用的系统，综合运用动态平均场理论、随机矩阵理论与数值模拟进行分析。

Result: 即使最小的无序量也会破坏传统逻辑斯蒂映射的周期倍增级联，系统转而发生向高维混沌的转变，并表现出一种在此前无序系统中未报道的振荡不稳定性；当映射间存在足够强的均匀耦合时，可恢复经典周期倍增级联的某些特征。

Conclusion: 动力学系统中已知的经典现象在无序面前可能十分脆弱；同时，无序耦合会催生既非低维简单动力学也非高维无序系统所具备的新现象。

Abstract: The logistic map is a quintessential model in the study of low-dimensional chaos. High-dimensional chaos, on the other hand, presents itself in disordered systems with many interacting and heterogeneously coupled components. Here, we formulate a system of many logistic maps, interacting through disordered couplings. Using a combination of dynamic mean-field theory, random matrix theory and numerical simulations, we show that even the smallest amount of disorder can remove the period-doubling cascade in the conventional logistic map. Instead we find a transition to high-dimensional chaos, marked by an oscillatory instability not previously reported for disordered systems. We also show that with sufficiently strong homogeneous coupling between the maps one recovers elements of the conventional period-doubling cascade. Our findings indicate that well-known phenomena in dynamical systems can be fragile in the face of disorder. At the same time, new phenomena emerge that are neither found in simple low-dimensional dynamics nor in high-dimensional disordered systems.

</details>
